% letters of a geometrical figure, overlined as printed (the line stands 9.3-9.7 pt above the baseline)

\begin{center}\Large AL-BATTĀNĪ\par\vspace{4mm}\LARGE OPUS ASTRONOMICUM\par

\vspace{12mm}\noindent Singulae lineae paginarum impressarum singulis lineis huius editionis respondent, cum
divisionibus vocabulorum, signis et generibus litterarum (rectis, inclinatis, distantibus). Textus ex imaginibus
paginarum transcriptus est; litterae Graecae et Arabicae, numeri et signa in imaginibus amplificatis lecta sunt.
Haec transcriptio a nullo homine recognita est.\par

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0768-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L002}{}{\Indent De Albatenii sermone brevissime t. I, p. \textsc{xlv} egi; pauca notanda supersunt. Generis \textit{femi-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L003}{}{\textit{nini} sunt (praeter \ArabicRun{رجْل}, \ArabicRun{عين}, \ArabicRun{يد} in catalogo fixarum plus semel occurrentia, et \ArabicRun{اصبع} ac \ArabicRun{قوس}):}
\par\vspace{11.04pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{أُذْن}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٦}, {\arabicfont ٢٧١} bis.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{فخذ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٥}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٨} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٥٠}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٢}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٠}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٧٣}.}}}}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L005}{5}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{أرنب}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٦٩} pluries.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{قدم}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٠}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٩} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢}.}}}}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{دراع}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٨} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢}.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{كتف}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٧}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٨}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٠}, {\arabicfont ٢٥١} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٥٢}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٣}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٣} bis,}}}}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L007}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{ساق}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥١}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٢}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٣} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٦٤}.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.87mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٦٧} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٧١} bis.}}}}
\par\vspace{3.54pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L008}{}{\textit{Masculini} generis vero:}
\par\vspace{3.45pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{جناح}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٩} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٥٩} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٧١} bis.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{قرن}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٦} quinquies (fem. {\arabicfont ٢٦٢}).}}}}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{ساعد}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٧}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٨}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢}.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{مرفق}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٤٧}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٨}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٧}.}}}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{ضِلْع}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٣} ter, {\arabicfont ٢٥٩}.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.75mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{منكب}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.52mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٣}.}}}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L012}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.61mm}\smash{\llap{\ArabicRun{عرقوب}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{15.38mm}\smash{\rlap{{\arabicfont ٢٥٠} paen., {\arabicfont ٢٥١} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٥٤} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢}.}}}}
\par\vspace{8.78pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0768-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L013}{}{\Indent Collectivum \ArabicRun{دَرَجٌ} (e quo n. un. \ArabicRun{دَرَجَةٌ} fit) ab al-Battānī reliquisque astronomis semper tam-}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L014}{}{quam fem. sing. construitur; cfr. \Name{Nöldeke}, \textit{Zur Grammatik des klassischen Arabisch},}
\par\vspace{0.02pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L015}{15}{Wien 1896, p. 84.}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0768-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L016}{}{\Indent Nominativum dualis in \ArabicRun{يْن}, pluralis in \ArabicRun{ين} exire semper in numeralibus, non raro in sub-}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L017}{}{stantivis et adiectivis, in cod. Escurialensi, monui t. I, p. \textsc{lxi}; quod cum potius amanuensi quam}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L018}{}{auctori tribuendum esset, in textu edendo emendavi (1). Sed nominativus \ArabicRun{سطري العدد} etc. persaepe}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L019}{}{et constanter in tabulis occurrens, procul dubio ita ab Albatenio ipso scriptus est; nam idem}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L020}{20}{usus apud ceteros astronomos et mathematicos viget. — Concordantiam numeralium cum ge-}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L021}{}{nere substantivorum mendosam, quae saepe in codice reperitur, nolui servare, cum vix ab}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L022}{}{Albatenio proveniat. — De \ArabicRun{اوّلة} v. Glossarium s. v. — Memoratu dignae fractiones sexagesimae}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L023}{}{inferiorum ordinum, p. {\arabicfont ١٠} in tabella contentae, ac potissimum \ArabicRun{عواشرين} « sexagesimae ordinis}
\par\vspace{0.02pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0768-body-L024}{}{« vicesimi ».}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0769-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L001}{}{\Indent Litterae quibus puncta, lineae, arcus, figurae geometricae designantur, in genetivo epe-}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L002}{}{xegetico (\ArabicRun{اضافة تفسيريّة}) censentur; ergo \ArabicRun{قوْسَا \ArOver{اب} و \ArOver{ج د}}, \ArabicRun{خطُّ \ArOver{ب ج}} etc., ut semper apud ma-}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L003}{}{thematicos. — Annexiones impropriae quas grammatici in numeris cardinalibus memorant (1),}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L004}{}{etiam cum fractionibus invenimus: \ArabicRun{الربْع يوم} {\arabicfont ٦٢}\textsubscript{12}, {\arabicfont ١٩١}\textsubscript{6}; \ArabicRun{الربْع اليوم} {\arabicfont ٦١}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٦٢}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{5, 18, 21}, {\arabicfont ١٩٣}\textsubscript{4}; \ArabicRun{الخُمْسَيْ}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L005}{5}{\ArabicRun{ساعة} {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{18}; \ArabicRun{الثُّلْثَيْ يوم والربْع يوم} {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{3}; \ArabicRun{النصف والربْع ساعة} {\arabicfont ٦٥}\textsubscript{9-10}; \ArabicRun{الثُّلْث عُشْرٍ} {\arabicfont ٩١}\textsubscript{21}; \ArabicRun{ذلك النصف السدس}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L006}{}{{\arabicfont ١٣٢}\textsubscript{2} (2). — Aliae quoque constructiones vix regulares cum numeris occurrunt: \ArabicRun{تلك السبعة}}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L007}{}{\ArabicRun{الاجزاء وثلثي جزء} {\arabicfont ١٨٢}\textsubscript{18}; \ArabicRun{على المائتين وواحد ونصف} {\arabicfont ١٨٣}\textsubscript{5}; \ArabicRun{على المائتين والواحد ونصف} {\arabicfont ١٨٤}\textsubscript{15}; \ArabicRun{على المائتين}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L008}{}{\ArabicRun{وواحد ونصف الذي هو قُطْر الارض} {\arabicfont ١٨٥}\textsubscript{3}. Interdum numeri per se ipsos determinati censentur, ar-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L009}{}{ticulo omisso: \ArabicRun{فٱضْرِبْها في ثلثة اجزاء وثماني دقائق ونصف التي هي قدر} {\arabicfont ١٤٩}\textsubscript{14}; \ArabicRun{وٱقْسِمْه على سبعة}}
\par\vspace{10.05pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L010}{10}{\ArabicRun{وسبعين التي هي قطر الظلّ} {\arabicfont ١٤٩}\textsubscript{16}; \ArabicRun{فزِدْها على احدى وثلثين اصبعًا وخُمْس اصبع التي هي اقلّ قطر الظلّ}}
\par\vspace{-0.07pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L011}{}{{\arabicfont ١٤٩}\textsubscript{17}. — Distributiva per numeri repetitionem multifarie exprimuntur: \ArabicRun{اربعةً اربعةً} {\arabicfont ١٩٣}\textsubscript{5}; \ArabicRun{سبعةً}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L012}{}{\ArabicRun{سبعةً} {\arabicfont ١٠١}\textsubscript{15, 20}, {\arabicfont ١٠٢}\textsubscript{3, 6, 9, 13, 16}; \ArabicRun{سبعةً بسبعةٍ} {\arabicfont ١١٠}\textsubscript{10}; \ArabicRun{درجةً بدرجة} {\arabicfont ٢٠}\textsubscript{22}, {\arabicfont ١١٣}\textsubscript{9}; \ArabicRun{لدرجة درجة} {\arabicfont ١٨}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٤٠}\textsubscript{4};}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L013}{}{\ArabicRun{المتفاضلة بعشرين عشرين} {\arabicfont ١٠٦}\textsubscript{9}; \ArabicRun{المتفاضلة ثلاثين ثلاثين} {\arabicfont ١٠٧}\textsubscript{6}; \ArabicRun{المتفاضلين بستّة ستّة اجزاء} {\arabicfont ١٧٣}\textsubscript{13};}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L014}{}{\ArabicRun{المتفاضليْن بستّة اجزاء ستّة اجزاء} {\arabicfont ١٧٤}; \ArabicRun{تفاضُل عشرة عشرة اجزاء} {\arabicfont ٢٢٥}\textsubscript{17}; \ArabicRun{ثلثين ثلثين} {\arabicfont ٢١٩}\textsubscript{7}; \ArabicRun{وأَسْقِطْها}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L015}{15}{\ArabicRun{مائتين وعشر مائتين وعشر} {\arabicfont ٢١٨}\textsubscript{12}; \ArabicRun{فأَلْقِهِ ثمانية وعشرين ثمانية وعشرين} {\arabicfont ٢١٩}\textsubscript{16}.}
\par\vspace{5.80pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0769-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L016}{}{\Indent Genetivus a duobus vel tribus nominibus simul pendens invenitur non raro in fractionibus}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L017}{}{designandis, licet regularior constructio frequens sit: \ArabicRun{نصف ورُبْع ساعة} {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ٦٥}\textsubscript{2}, {\arabicfont ٨٦}\textsubscript{13}; \ArabicRun{نصف ورُبْع}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{دقيقة} {\arabicfont ٦٨}\textsubscript{10}; \ArabicRun{نصف ورُبْع جزء} {\arabicfont ٨٣}\textsubscript{3}; \ArabicRun{نصف ورُبْع يوم} {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{2, 16}; \ArabicRun{نصف وثُلْث يوم} {\arabicfont ٦٣}\textsubscript{1}; \ArabicRun{نصف وثُلْث ساعة}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L019}{}{{\arabicfont ٨٥}\textsubscript{21}; \ArabicRun{نصف وثُلْث قُطْره} {\arabicfont ٨٦}\textsubscript{7}, {\arabicfont ٨٧}\textsubscript{1}; \ArabicRun{ثُلْث ورُبْع ساعة} {\arabicfont ٨٦}\textsubscript{17}; \ArabicRun{خُمْس وسُدْس يوم} {\arabicfont ١٠١}\textsubscript{7}, {\arabicfont ١٠٤}\textsubscript{10}; \ArabicRun{بسُدْس وثُمْن}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L020}{20}{\ArabicRun{ساعة} {\arabicfont ٨٦}\textsubscript{17}; \ArabicRun{خُمْس وسُدْس يوم} {\arabicfont ١٠١}\textsubscript{7}, {\arabicfont ١٠٤}\textsubscript{10}; \ArabicRun{بسُدْس وثُمْن الفضل} {\arabicfont ١٤٤}\textsubscript{7, 14}; \ArabicRun{نصف وسُدْس دقيقة} {\arabicfont ١٨٢}\textsubscript{19}; \ArabicRun{نصف}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L021}{}{\ArabicRun{وثُلْث وثُمْن قطره} {\arabicfont ٨٦}\textsubscript{12}. Sed praeter fractionum usum, haec tantum exempla rei inveni: \ArabicRun{مطالع}}
\par\vspace{2.82pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L022}{}{\ArabicRun{ومغارب البروج} {\arabicfont ١٢٩}\textsubscript{11}, {\arabicfont ١٣٠}\textsubscript{11}, {\arabicfont ١٣٢}\textsubscript{14}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٣}\textsubscript{1}; \ArabicRun{سمت مطلع ومغيب غير هاتين النقطتين} {\arabicfont ٢٩}\textsubscript{18}; \ArabicRun{سمت مطلع}}
\par\vspace{1.74pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L023}{}{\ArabicRun{ومغيب ايّ درجة شِئْتَ} {\arabicfont ٣٠}\textsubscript{1} (3).}
\par\vspace{3.09pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0769-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L024}{}{\Indent \ArabicRun{مَا} « quilibet, quicumque, quodlibet » etc., haud infrequens apud mathematicos, raro adhi-}
\par\vspace{-0.79pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L025}{25}{betur: \ArabicRun{دائرة ما} {\arabicfont ١٣}\textsubscript{18}.}
\par\vspace{3.36pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0769-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L026}{}{\Indent Bis \ArabicRun{مِنْ} occurrit ubi simplex genetivus exspectandus: \ArabicRun{التي هي قدر الدائرة من القمر} « quae}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L027}{}{« est quantitas disci lunaris » ({\arabicfont ١٤٩}\textsubscript{14}); \ArabicRun{فهو سهم الدائرة من الظلّ} « ipsa fit sagitta circuli umbrae »}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L028}{}{({\arabicfont ١٥٠}\textsubscript{3-4}). Sed fortasse id ab amanuensi provenit qui \ArabicRun{الدائرة} cum articulo incaute scriptum delere}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L029}{}{noluit, et per \ArabicRun{من} rectam constructionem servare putavit. — Ad designandam comparationem}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L030}{30}{loci adhibetur p. {\arabicfont ٢٧٣}: \ArabicRun{المقدّم من هذا} « quae hanc praecedit », \ArabicRun{الكوكب الباقي الجنوبيّ من هذا}}
\par\vspace{-0.52pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L031}{}{« stella reliqua ab austro huius ».}
\par\vspace{3.09pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0769-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L032}{}{\Indent Memoratu digna superlativa \ArabicRun{اطول ما يكون} {\arabicfont ٣٢}\textsubscript{14}; \ArabicRun{اقصر ما يكون} ibid.; \ArabicRun{اتمّ ما يكون} {\arabicfont ٨٣}\textsubscript{11}, {\arabicfont ١٤٦}\textsubscript{13};}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-body-L033}{}{\ArabicRun{اكثر ما يكون} {\arabicfont ٩٥}\textsubscript{9}; de quibus v. \Name{Fleischer}, \textit{Klein. Schr.}, I, 475-477.}

\par\vspace{0.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0769-notes_left-L006}{}{grammaticos); \Name{Fleischer}, \textit{Klein. Schr.}, I, 624-}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0770-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L001}{}{\Indent \ArabicRun{قد} saepe imperfecto praeponitur; tunc per verbum « posse » optime vertitur (1): \ArabicRun{وقد}}
\par\vspace{0.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L002}{}{\ArabicRun{تكتفي} « potes satis habere », « tibi sufficere potest » {\arabicfont ١٧}\textsubscript{5}, {\arabicfont ٤٠}\textsubscript{3}; \ArabicRun{وقد تعرف} « potes etiam cogno-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L003}{}{scere » {\arabicfont ٣٤}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ٥٠}\textsubscript{11}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٨}\textsubscript{20}; \ArabicRun{وقد يُعْرَف} « potest etiam cognosci » {\arabicfont ٤٣}\textsubscript{4}; \ArabicRun{وقد يمكن} « fieri etiam potest »}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L004}{}{{\arabicfont ١٠٠}\textsubscript{3}, {\arabicfont ١٣٦}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ١٤٦}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{2}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٥}\textsubscript{15}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٩}\textsubscript{8, 13, 14}; \ArabicRun{قد يجوز أن} « fieri potest ut » {\arabicfont ١٣٣}\textsubscript{4}; \ArabicRun{قد يكسِف} « potest}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L005}{5}{tegere » {\arabicfont ٨٣}\textsubscript{20, 22}; \ArabicRun{وقد يُقال} « et potest appellari » {\arabicfont ١٧٩}\textsubscript{17}. — Alia exempla {\arabicfont ١٨٨}\textsubscript{19}, {\arabicfont ١٩١}\textsubscript{16}, {\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{6, 10}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٠}\textsubscript{17},}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L006}{}{{\arabicfont ٢٠٩}\textsubscript{21, 22}, {\arabicfont ٢١٨}\textsubscript{3}. — Sive verbo « posse » sive adverbio « interdum » (2) bene vertitur {\arabicfont ١٣٠}\textsubscript{15}, {\arabicfont ١٣٣}\textsubscript{8, 10}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0770-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L007}{}{\Indent \ArabicRun{إِلَّا أَنْ} « nisi quatenus » (Italice « a meno che », Anglice « unless ») {\arabicfont ١٠٢}\textsubscript{2}, {\arabicfont ١١٩}\textsubscript{20}, {\arabicfont ١٣٣}\textsubscript{3}, {\arabicfont ١٤٠}\textsubscript{2};}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L008}{}{cfr. \Name{Lane}, in fine v. \ArabicRun{لا} et \Name{Reckendorf}, p. 719. — Sensu negativo \ArabicRun{إنْ} occurrit {\arabicfont ١٢٥}\textsubscript{7}: \ArabicRun{وإنْ}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L009}{}{\ArabicRun{يتهيّأ أَنْ يكون اكثرَ منها إِلّا اذا كان.....}.}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0770-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L010}{10}{\Indent Saepe \ArabicRun{أَنْ} pro \ArabicRun{لك أَنْ} adhibetur: ... \ArabicRun{وإن شئتَ أن تعرف خطّ ما بين} « et si mavis potes}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L011}{}{« cognoscere lineam inter ... » ({\arabicfont ٣٦}\textsubscript{13}); ... \ArabicRun{وإن شئتَ أن تنظر الى الفضل الذي} « et si mavis, po-}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L012}{}{« tes residuum considerare quod ... » ({\arabicfont ٤١}\textsubscript{14}). Alia exempla {\arabicfont ٤٥}\textsubscript{12}, {\arabicfont ٥٠}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ٦٧}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ١٤٧}\textsubscript{20}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0770-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L013}{}{\Indent Quoad verborum ordinem in periodo cfr. {\arabicfont ٦٠}\textsubscript{11}: \ArabicRun{وَتَرُ نصفِ ما بينهما من اجزاء الطول المنصَّفُ}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L014}{}{« chorda dimidiata graduum longitudinis inter eas intercedentium »; — {\arabicfont ١٢١}\textsubscript{10}: \ArabicRun{في اختلاف منظر}}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L015}{15}{\ArabicRun{القمر في دائرة الارتفاع هذا} « in hanc parallaxim lunarem altitudinis »; — {\arabicfont ١٣٠}\textsubscript{2}: \ArabicRun{فعلم المقدار في}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L016}{}{\ArabicRun{اقليم واحد واذا عُلم ذلك في اقليم واحد كان ذلك معلومًا في سائر الاقاليم هو الذي تجتمع آراء الناس عليه...}}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L017}{}{« cognitio ergo huius quantitatis in uno climatum (et si in uno climatum cognoscitur etiam}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L018}{}{« in reliquis nota fit) est id in quo opiniones hominum versantur ... ». Alia perplexarum pe-}
\par\vspace{0.29pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L019}{}{riodorum exempla in I capite et p. {\arabicfont ١٩٨}\textsubscript{6-10}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0770-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L020}{20}{\Indent Memoratu digna sequentia: \ArabicRun{فٱعرفْ وَتَر نصف قوس النهار راجعا} « deprehende sinum versum}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L021}{}{« arcus semidiurni » ({\arabicfont ٤٥}\textsubscript{1-2}); — \ArabicRun{فاعرف وتره راجعًا} « eius sinum versum cognosce » ({\arabicfont ٤٦}\textsubscript{10}); — \ArabicRun{اعْرفْ}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L022}{}{\ArabicRun{وتر نصف قوس نهار الكوكب راجعًا} « sinum versum arcus semidiurni stellae deprehende » ({\arabicfont ٥١}\textsubscript{4});}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L023}{}{— \ArabicRun{فاعرفْ وتر هذا البعد راجعًا} « sinum versum huius distantiae cognosce » ({\arabicfont ٥٣}\textsubscript{6}); — \ArabicRun{ثمّ ٱعرفْ وتر}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L024}{}{\ArabicRun{عرض الكوكب تامًّا} « postea chordam integram latitudinis stellae cognosce » ({\arabicfont ١٩٧}\textsubscript{5}). Hi \ArabicRun{راجعًا} et}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L025}{25}{\ArabicRun{تامًّا} (3) accusativi sunt adverbiales, non \ArabicRun{الحال}, ut Arabum grammatici aiunt, qui ad subiectum}
\par\vspace{0.11pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L026}{}{verbi referantur; quod melius etiam nonnulla loca ex \Name{Ḥabash} (4) demonstrabunt: f. 81,r.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L027}{}{(l. 3 a f.) \ArabicRun{هو قوس الجيب معكوسًا} « isque est arcus versus sinus »; — ibid. (l. 2 a f.) \ArabicRun{وأردنا}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L028}{}{\ArabicRun{قوسه معكوسًا}; — ibid. (l. 1 a f.) \ArabicRun{هو قوس الجيب الزائدة على ص معكوسًا} « est arcus sinus versi}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L029}{}{« excedens 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} »; — f. 99,v. (l. 4 a f.) \ArabicRun{ناخذ قوسه معكوسًا}; — f. 100,r. (l. 4 a f.) \ArabicRun{اخذنا جيبه}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L030}{30}{\ArabicRun{معكوسًا}; — ibid. (l. 3 a f.) \ArabicRun{وهو جيب قوس النهار معكوسًا لذلك اليوم} « estque sinus versus arcus}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L031}{}{« diurni illius diei »; — f. 101,r. (paen.) \ArabicRun{فٱجْعَلْهُ قوسًا مستويًا} « eum recte arcua »; — f. 102,v.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L032}{}{(l. 9) \ArabicRun{فهو جيب ناخذ قوسَهُ معكوسًا} « isque erit sinus cuius arcum versum sumemus »; —}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L033}{}{f. 107,r. (l. 9) \ArabicRun{فهو جيب معكوس ناخذ قوسه معكوسًا} « eritque sinus versus cuius arcum versum}
\par\vspace{-0.07pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0770-body-L034}{}{« sumemus ».}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L001}{}{\ArabicRun{البُطَيْنُ} secunda statio lunaris complectens \textgreek{ε, δ, ρ}′ \textit{Arietis}, {\arabicfont ١٨٨}\textsubscript{21}. Iuxta \Name{Ideler}, \textgreek{ε, δ, π}; iuxta}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Lane} \textgreek{ε, π, ν}; iuxta notationem \Name{Schjellerup}, \textgreek{ε, δ, ρ}. Cfr. quae in additamentis huius}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tomi de nonnullis stationibus lunaribus expono.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L004}{}{\ArabicRun{بَعُدَ} et \ArabicRun{بُعْد} in codice cum \ArabicRun{من} et cum \ArabicRun{عن} construuntur indifferenter; in textu edendo \ArabicRun{عن} re-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cepi. — \ArabicRun{بُعْد} pluralem habet \ArabicRun{أَبْعَاد} saepe occurrentem; cfr. \textit{Mafātīḥ}, 203 \ArabicRun{الابعاد} « tres di-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}mensiones » i. e. longitudo, latitudo, altitudo. \Name{Dozy} pluralem ex Abū ’l-Walīd cognoscit;}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Friedländer}, \textit{Arab.-deutsch. Lex. zum Sprachgebrauch des Maimonides}, Frank-}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}furt a. M. 1902, 9,\textit{b}. — \ArabicRun{البُعْد عن فلك معدِّل النهار} \textit{declinatio} puncti sphaerae caelestis}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}aut sideris quod in ecliptica non iaceat (si in ecliptica est, eius declinatio \ArabicRun{مَيْل} vocatur)}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٤٦}, {\arabicfont ٤٧} ac persaepe; diversitas a \ArabicRun{مَيْل} optime ex {\arabicfont ٥٣}\textsubscript{21-22} patet. — \ArabicRun{البُعْد الأَبْعَد من الفلك}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الخارج المركز} vel tantum \ArabicRun{البعد الابعد} \textgreek{ἀπόγειον τοῦ ἐκκέντρου}, \textit{apogeum excentrici} Solis, Lu-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}nae, planetarum, {\arabicfont ٦٧}\textsubscript{1, 12}, {\arabicfont ٩٥}–{\arabicfont ٩٦}, {\arabicfont ١٧٢}–{\arabicfont ١٧٣} etc.; idem quod \ArabicRun{اوج} aliorum astronomorum. — \ArabicRun{البعد}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الاقرب} \textgreek{περίγειον τοῦ ἐκκέντρου}, \textit{perigeum excentrici} Solis, Lunae, planetarum {\arabicfont ٧٠}\textsubscript{2}, {\arabicfont ٩٦}\textsubscript{11},}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{18} etc. — \ArabicRun{البعد الابعد في فلك التدوير} \textgreek{ἀπόγειον τοῦ ἐπικύκλου}, \textit{apogeum epicycli} Lunae,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}planetarum {\arabicfont ٩٦}\textsubscript{19}, etc. — (seu \ArabicRun{الحقيقيّ}) \ArabicRun{البعد الابعد الحقّيّ} \textgreek{τὸ ἀκριβὲς ἀπόγειον}, \textit{apogeum}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{verum epicycli} Lunae ({\arabicfont ٧٨}\textsubscript{4}), planetarum ({\arabicfont ٩٦}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{2, 8}). — \ArabicRun{البعد الابعد الاوسط} \textgreek{τὸ μέσον}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἀπόγειον}, \textit{apogeum medium epicycli} Lunae et planetarum, {\arabicfont ٩٦}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{2, 9}. — \ArabicRun{بُعْد القمر المُضْعَف}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(\ArabicRun{عن الشمس}), \textgreek{ἡ μέση ἀποχὴ τοῦ ἡλίου καὶ τῆς σελήνης διπλασιασϑεῖσα}, \textit{dupla elongatio Lu-}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{nae mediae a Sole medio}, {\arabicfont ٨٠}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ٨٢}\textsubscript{12} etc. — \ArabicRun{البعد عن خطّ وَسَط السماء} \textit{angulus hora-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{rius} aut \textit{eius complementum} {\arabicfont ١٢٤}\textsubscript{20}, {\arabicfont ١٩٨} seqq.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L021}{}{\ArabicRun{البَقَّارُ} \textgreek{ὁ Βοώτης}, constellatio \textit{Bootis} (nomen Arabicum versio Graeci) {\arabicfont ٢٤٧}. \Name{Dozy} ex Ellyūs}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Boqṭor.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L023}{}{\ArabicRun{البَلْدَةُ} vigesima prima statio lunaris complectens regiunculam Caeli stellis destitutam, sub \textgreek{ξ, ο},}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{π}, \textit{d}, \textgreek{ρ, υ} Sagittarii, {\arabicfont ١٨٩}\textsubscript{5}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L025}{25}{\ArabicRun{بلوقامس} \textgreek{ὁ Πλόκαμος}, constellatio \textit{Comae Berenices} {\arabicfont ٢٥٨}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L026}{}{\ArabicRun{بُهْتٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{أَبْهَاتٌ} \textit{motus verus} (seu \textit{inaequalis}) Solis, Lunae, planetarum \textit{in die} (nychthemero);}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vox Albatenio ignota, de qua t. I, p. \textsc{xliii}, n. 2 egi. Definitio \textit{Tāǵ al-‘arūs}, I, 529, l. 10}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}a f. (\ArabicRun{والبهت حساب من حساب النجوم وهو مسيرها المستوي في يوم}), quam \Name{Lane} sequitur,}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vera fit si pro \ArabicRun{النجوم} et \ArabicRun{المستوي} ponatur \ArabicRun{الكواكب السيّارة} et \ArabicRun{المختلف}. Recte \Name{Ḥabash},}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}f. 113,r.: « \textit{buht} errantium siderum est ipsorum iter (\ArabicRun{مسير}) inaequale pro die (\ArabicRun{ليوم}) »;}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cfr. \Name{at–Tahānawī}, \textit{Dictionary}, ed. Sprenger, p. 1553. Si motus verus (vel inaequalis)}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}spatio decem aut quinque dierum dividatur per 10 aut 5, exit \textit{buht medius} (\ArabicRun{اوسط}); si}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}autem hic \textit{buht} ita corrigatur ut fiat motus verus in nychthemero dato, habetur \textit{buht}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{aequatus} \ArabicRun{البهت المعدّل}. V. \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 113,r.–v.; \Name{Oloug Beg}, p. 116–120 text. = 161–}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}165 vers. (ubi « vitesse »); \textit{Mafātīḥ}, 223 (ubi tantum: \ArabicRun{البُهْت المعدَّل هو سير الكوكب}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{المعدَّل ليوم وليلة}). Pluralis \ArabicRun{أَبْهَات} occurrit etiam in adpendice ignoti auctoris ad Tabulas}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L037}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Ibn ash–Shāṭir} (Catal. Paris., n. 2522) et \Name{Oloug Beg}, p. 118, l. 1 et 2 text. Vox}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L038}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Persica ex Indico \textit{bhukti}, cuius forma Prākritica \textit{bhuttī} (v. \Name{Sachau} ad \Name{al-Bīrūnī}, \textit{In-}}
\par\vspace{2.73pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L039}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{dia}, vers., t. II, p. 346).}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0773-E07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0773-body-L040}{40}{\ArabicRun{بَيْتٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{أَبْيَاتٌ} et \ArabicRun{بُيُوتٌ} \textit{areola} (Italice « casella ») quadrata in tabulis per lineas aequidistantes}
\DSource{0774}{325}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}verticales et horizontales divisis, {\arabicfont ٩}\textsubscript{20, 21, 22}, {\arabicfont ١٠}\textsubscript{20}, {\arabicfont ٢١٠}\textsubscript{19, 20, 21}, {\arabicfont ٢١١}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ٢١٢}\textsubscript{1, 10, 11}, {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{6, 7}. Cfr.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Dorn}, \textit{Drei arab. astron. Instrum.}, p. 16 et 117; \Name{Dozy}, I, 131,\textit{b} (ex Ibn Khaldūn et}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}serioribus scriptoribus). — \ArabicRun{بَيْتٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{بُيُوتٌ} \textgreek{τόπος}, una e \textit{duodecim domibus caelestibus}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ١٠٩}–{\arabicfont ١١١}. — \ArabicRun{بَيْت} pl. \ArabicRun{بيوت} \textgreek{οἶκος} (aliis \textgreek{τόπος}), \textit{domicilium} Solis, Lunae, planetarum, iuxta}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}astrologos, {\arabicfont ٢٤٤} (tabula spuria).}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L006}{}{\ArabicRun{بَيْضَةٌ} \textgreek{στερεὰ σφαῖρα}, \textit{globus caelestis}, quo stellarum situs indicantur {\arabicfont ٥}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٩} in titulo, {\arabicfont ٢١٠}\textsubscript{3, 4}.}
\par\vspace{2.41pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}V. \textit{Mafātīḥ}, 235; \Name{‘Umar ibn Muḥ. al-Kindī} (1), \textit{Faḍā’il Miṣr}, ed. Oestrup, p. 192}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(= vers. 223); \Name{al-Ya‘qūbī}, \textit{Hist.}, ed. Houtsma, I, 150.}
\par\vspace{2.48pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L009}{}{\ArabicRun{بين} — Termino \ArabicRun{ما بَيْنَ} vel \ArabicRun{فيما بَيْنَ} respondet \ArabicRun{الى} ({\arabicfont ١٧}\textsubscript{6}, {\arabicfont ٢٧}\textsubscript{14}, {\arabicfont ٣٩}\textsubscript{17, 23}, {\arabicfont ٤٠}\textsubscript{3, 9, 10, 11, 20, 21}, {\arabicfont ٥١}\textsubscript{10, 11},}
\par\vspace{2.31pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٥٦}\textsubscript{2, 4}, {\arabicfont ٦٤}\textsubscript{22}, {\arabicfont ٦٦}\textsubscript{2}, {\arabicfont ٨٢}\textsubscript{14}, {\arabicfont ١١٥}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ١٦١}\textsubscript{1}) aut simplex \ArabicRun{و} ({\arabicfont ٢٩}\textsubscript{7-8}, {\arabicfont ٣٥}\textsubscript{8, 10}, {\arabicfont ٣٦}\textsubscript{13, 14}); sed simplici \ArabicRun{بَيْنَ}}
\par\vspace{2.45pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}aliud \ArabicRun{بَيْنَ} respondet ({\arabicfont ١٧٧}\textsubscript{13, 15, 16}, {\arabicfont ١٩٢}\textsubscript{1, 4} etc.). Qua re fortasse emendanda quae habet \Name{Re-}}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{ckendorf}, \textit{Syntakt. Verh.}, p. 239-240.}
\par\vspace{2.15pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L013}{}{\ArabicRun{تام} — \ArabicRun{التَّوْءمَانِ} \textgreek{οἱ Δίδυμοι}, constellatio \textit{Geminorum} {\arabicfont ١٨٨}\textsubscript{22}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٦} (in Catalogo fixarum ex \textit{vetere}}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Almagesti versione}); sed alibi semper nomen Arabicum \ArabicRun{الجَوْزَاء} auctor adhibet. — Cfr.}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Lane} ex al-Qazwīnī.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L016}{}{\ArabicRun{التربه} (?) \textgreek{υ}\textsuperscript{5} \textit{Eridani} {\arabicfont ٢٦٩} (cfr. t. II, p. 170, n.).}
\par\vspace{2.14pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L017}{}{\ArabicRun{تَلَا} u, de sideribus, \textgreek{ἕπεσθαι}, \textit{sequi}, i. e. ab oriente alterius esse, maiorem quam aliud sidus lon-}
\par\vspace{2.53pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}gitudinem habere (2), passim in catalogo fixarum {\arabicfont ٢٤٥}–{\arabicfont ٢٧٤}. — \ArabicRun{التَّالي} i. q. \ArabicRun{المؤَخَّر} \textgreek{ἑπόμενος},}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{sequens} sidus, ab oriente alterius, passim in catal. fixarum. - - \ArabicRun{الجِهَةُ التَّاليَةُ} ({\arabicfont ٦٨}\textsubscript{5, 21}, {\arabicfont ١٩٥}\textsubscript{18})}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}i. q. \ArabicRun{الى تَوَالي البُرُوج} seu \ArabicRun{الى جهة توالي البروج} \textgreek{εἰς τὰ ἑπόμενα τῶν ζωδίων}, \textit{in consequentia},}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{iuxta ordinem} vel \textit{successionem signorum}, i. e. ab occasu in ortum versus, {\arabicfont ٦٨}\textsubscript{3, 4},}
\par\vspace{2.26pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٧٦}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ٧٧}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ٩٥}\textsubscript{13, 14}, {\arabicfont ٩٨}\textsubscript{17}, {\arabicfont ١١١}\textsubscript{9}. Eius contrarium \ArabicRun{الى خِلاف توالي البروج} ({\arabicfont ٦٨}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٧٦}\textsubscript{19}. {\arabicfont ٧٧}\textsubscript{7}) i. q.}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الجهة المتقدّمة}.}
\par\vspace{2.54pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L024}{}{\ArabicRun{تَمَام} \textgreek{αἱ λοιπαὶ εἰς τὸ τεταρτημόριον μοῖραι}, \textit{complementum} sensu geometrico ac trigonometrico,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}i. e. quod quantitati angulari datae deest ad perficiendos 90°; \ArabicRun{تمام الارتفاع الى رُبْع الدائرة}}
\par\vspace{2.53pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{complementum altitudinis ad 90°} {\arabicfont ٣٧}\textsubscript{17} ac persaepe.}
\par\vspace{2.16pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-E07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L027}{}{\ArabicRun{ثبت} — IV \textit{scribere, notare}; {\arabicfont ١٠٦}\textsubscript{13} \ArabicRun{فأَثْبِتْ كلّ جنس تحت جنسه تحت الذي أَثْبَتَّ اوَّلًا}; {\arabicfont ١٠٦}\textsubscript{15}}
\par\vspace{2.31pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{وأثْبِتْ تلك الدرج والدقائق والثواني تحت الذي أثْبَتَّه قَبْلُ كلَّ جنس تحت جنسه}; item {\arabicfont ٦٤}\textsubscript{6},}
\par\vspace{2.26pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٧٥}\textsubscript{17}, {\arabicfont ١٠٦}\textsubscript{11, 18, 19, 21}, {\arabicfont ١٠٧}\textsubscript{1, 10, 13}, {\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{5, 10, 11, 12, 13}, {\arabicfont ١٢٠} pluries, etc. Cfr. \Name{Ibn Ǵubayr}, editio}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Wright {\arabicfont ٢١٤}\textsubscript{4-5}: \ArabicRun{وهذه الاثار الكريمة تَلَقَّيْنَاها من أَلْسِنَة اشياخ من اهل البلد فاثبتناها حسبما}}
\par\vspace{2.25pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{نقلوها الينا والله اعلم بصحة ذلك} « has venerabiles traditiones ex ore quorumdam seniorum}
\par\vspace{2.39pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« urbis collegimus, et [hoc nostro diario] perscripsimus eo modo quo nobis traditae sunt ... ».}
\par\vspace{2.10pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Congruit fere cum peculiari sensu a \Name{Lane}, I, 329,\textit{a} recepto (\textit{He set it down, registered}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{it}, or \textit{recorded it}, i. e., a man’s name, \ArabicRun{في الديوان} \textit{in the register of soldiers} or \textit{pensioners}}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}or \textit{accounts}), quod apud \Name{Cusa}, \textit{I diplomi greci ed arabi di Sicilia}, t. I, p. 625, l. 4}
\par\vspace{2.41pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}a f. occurrit \ArabicRun{وأَثْبَتُوه حيث يجبُ اثباته من دفاتر الايصال الديوانية}. Ceterum his diplomatibus}
\par\vspace{11.14pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{7.98pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\vspace*{0.45pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Floruit posteriore IV saec. heg. (X Chr.)}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_left-L002}{}{medietate; libellum excerpsit ex libro ampliore pa-}
\par\vspace{0.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_left-L003}{}{tris sui Abū ‘Umar Muḥammad al-Kindī, quem plus}
\par\vspace{0.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_left-L004}{}{semel laudat. Perperam ergo \Name{Wüstenfeld}, et}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_right-L001}{}{\Name{Brockelmann}, I, 149 libelli auctorem dicunt}
\par\vspace{0.61pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_right-L002}{}{Abū ‘Umar Muḥammad, ut monui in \textit{Cosmos di}}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_right-L003}{}{\textit{G. Cora}, t. XIII, 1901, p. 40.}
\par\vspace{0.61pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0774-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0774-notes_right-L004}{}{\Indent (2) Qua re serius per meridianum transit.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0775}{326}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}XII saeculi Chr. frequentior I forma; \Name{Cusa}, p. 82, l. 5 \ArabicRun{وثبتت اسماهم في اخر هذا السجل}}
\par\vspace{2.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}= p. 80 \textgreek{ὧν τὰ ὀνόματα κατωτέρως γραφήσονται}; p. 104, l. 5 a f. \ArabicRun{وثبت عندي ما ذكر من}}
\par\vspace{2.59pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{ثبات توكيل وقبول}; p. 105, l. 4 \ArabicRun{وثبت شهادتهما عندي}; p. 516 \ArabicRun{ثبت حدود هذه على الشرح}}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{ما هو مثبوت في هذا السجل بالرومي} = \textgreek{ταῦτ’ οὖν οὕτω διαχωρισϑέντα καὶ διορησϑέντα ἐγγρα}-}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{φῶς ἐν τῷδε τοῦ κράτους ἡμῶν σιγιλλίῳ}. Cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I, 157,\textit{a} (sub I et \ArabicRun{ثابت}). — \ArabicRun{البُرُوجُ}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الثَّابِتَةُ} \textgreek{ζώδια στερεά}, \textit{signa fixa} i. e. Taurus, Leo, Scorpius, Aquarius, in \textit{Tetrabibli ver-}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{sione}, v. t. I, p. \textsc{xxi}, n. 2.}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L008}{}{\ArabicRun{ثُخْنٌ} \textit{latitudo} superficiei exterioris armillae, {\arabicfont ٢١١}\textsubscript{12}: \ArabicRun{ثمّ تتّخذ حلقة من نحاس قائمة السطوح ...}}
\par\vspace{2.38pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{يكون سَمْكها بقدر عرض الابهام وثُخْنها مقدار ما تحتاج الى قوّته لكيلا تضطرب} quod \Name{Plato Ti-}}
\par\vspace{2.23pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{burtinus} ita vertit (cod. Bibliothecae Marcianae, f. 83,v., col. \textit{a–b}): « aeneum circulum}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« planae superficiei cuius latitudo sit unius digiti, \textit{grossitudo} vero sit mediocris ita tamen}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« quod nunquam curvari aut alterari possit ». Versio \textit{Hispanica}, f. 23,v.: « et desi toma}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« una armella de arambre ... que aya en su anchura tanto como un dedo y que sea}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« su \textit{gordura} deguisa que non se ajetra ». In sequentibus auctor semper \ArabicRun{غِلْظ} pro \ArabicRun{ثخن}}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}adhibet. Res notanda, quia apud astronomos plerumque \ArabicRun{ثخن} est quod ab al-Battānī \ArabicRun{سَمْك}}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vocatur; \Name{al-Qazwīnī} ed. Wüst., t. I, p. 17 ult. (= ed. Kahir. I, 26) \ArabicRun{ثخن فلك القمر وهو}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{بعد ما بين سطحه الاعلى وسطحه الادنى}; \Name{al-Ǵaghmīnī}, p. 221 ult., etc.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{ثقل} — [\ArabicRun{ثَقِيلٌ}] pl. \ArabicRun{ثِقَالٌ} planeta \textit{altero tardior} in motu {\arabicfont ١٩٥}\textsubscript{2, 3}. Cfr. \Name{Sahl} ap. \Name{Loth}, \textit{al-Kindî}}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(Morgenl. Forsch. p. 256 n.); \Name{Alchabitium}, ed. 1485, f. cc5,r.–6,r. pluries (diff. III, c. 3\textsuperscript{o}).}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L020}{20}{\ArabicRun{ثلث} — \ArabicRun{ثَالِثَةٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{ثَوَالِثُ} \textit{tertia pars sexagesima, minutum tertium}, {\arabicfont ٨}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ٩}\textsubscript{5, 9, 10}, {\arabicfont ١٠}, {\arabicfont ١١}; et ita}
\par\vspace{2.41pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}mathematici omnes. — \ArabicRun{تَثْلِيثٌ} \textgreek{τὸ τρίγωνον}, \textit{trinus aspectus, trigonum} {\arabicfont ١٩٤}–{\arabicfont ١٩٧}; cfr. \Name{Dozy},}
\par\vspace{2.38pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}I, 162,\textit{b}. — \ArabicRun{المُثَلَّثُ} \textgreek{τὸ Τρίγωνον}, constellatio \textit{Trianguli} {\arabicfont ٢٥٤} (ex vetere Almagesti versione);}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I, 163,\textit{a}. — \ArabicRun{مُثَلَّثَةٌ} \textit{triangulum} geometricum, {\arabicfont ٣٢}, {\arabicfont ١٥٠} pluries, {\arabicfont ١٦٥} pluries, {\arabicfont ٢٠٦}\textsubscript{23};}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}masc. \ArabicRun{مُثَلَّثٌ} {\arabicfont ٣٨}, {\arabicfont ٦٩}, {\arabicfont ٧٠}, {\arabicfont ٧١}, {\arabicfont ٢٠٦}\textsubscript{21}. — [\ArabicRun{مُثَلَّثَة}] pl. \ArabicRun{مُثَلَّثَاتٌ} \textgreek{τὸ τρίγωνον}, \textit{trigonum, trique-}}
\par\vspace{2.23pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{trum, triplicitas astrologica}, in tabula spuria {\arabicfont ٢٤٤} bis (et t. II, p. 311); cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I,}
\par\vspace{2.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}162,\textit{b}, s. v. \ArabicRun{تثليث}.}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L027}{}{\ArabicRun{الجَاثي} \textgreek{ὁ ἐν γόνασιν} « nixus in genibus, ingeniculus », constellatio \textit{Herculis} {\arabicfont ٢٤٨}; cfr. \Name{Lane}.}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L028}{}{\ArabicRun{جَدْوَلَ} \textgreek{ἡ κανονοποιΐα}, \textit{in tabulis mathematicis describere, tabulas mathematicas conficere},}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« tabulare » ut scriptores aevi medii serioresque dicunt, {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{3} (ubi pro \ArabicRun{تَجَدْوَل} l. \ArabicRun{تُجَدْوَل}),}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٤٠}\textsubscript{4}, {\arabicfont ٢٢٦}\textsubscript{1}. — \ArabicRun{جَدْوَلٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{جَدَاوِلُ} \textit{columna} tabulae mathematicae, qua functiones argumenti}
\par\vspace{2.05pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}exhibentur (nam columnae argumentum exhibentes \ArabicRun{سطر} vocantur), ac \textit{tabula} ipsa (1), {\arabicfont ١٠}}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}pluries, {\arabicfont ١١} pluries, {\arabicfont ١٢}\textsubscript{19}, {\arabicfont ١٥}\textsubscript{22}, {\arabicfont ١٦}\textsubscript{3} etc. Cfr. \Name{Lane} ex TA; \Name{Dozy}, I, 175–176. — \ArabicRun{جُدَيْوِلٌ}}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{tabella} mathematica {\arabicfont ٢٢٦}\textsubscript{3}. Grammatici \ArabicRun{جُدَيِّل} malunt, sed \ArabicRun{جُدَيْوِل} sinunt; \Name{Fleischer},}
\par\vspace{2.18pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Klein. Schr.}, I, 222; \Name{Sībawayhi}, ed. Derenbourg, II, 132–133 (vers. Jahn, II, 437–38);}
\par\vspace{2.15pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{az-Zamakhsharī}, \textit{Mufaṣṣal}, ed. Bloch\textsuperscript{2}, p. 86 (ed. Kahir., 1323 heg., p. 204).}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L036}{}{\ArabicRun{جذر} — \ArabicRun{تَجْذيرٌ} \textit{radicis mathematicae extractio}, {\arabicfont ١}\textsubscript{15}, {\arabicfont ١٠}\textsubscript{24}, ut semper apud mathematicos. Sed}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L037}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}verbum finitum \ArabicRun{جَذَّرَ} al–Battānī et veteres scriptores non usurpant; dicunt enim \ArabicRun{أَخَذَ جِذْرَهُ},}
\par\vspace{1.65pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-body-L038}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{τὴν πλευρὰν λαμβάνειν}.}
\par\vspace{3.62pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{6.51pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0775-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-notes-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Ptolemaeus} tabulam vocat \textgreek{κανών} vel \textgreek{κανόνιον}, columnam (tam argumenti quam functionum)}
\par\vspace{-1.20pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0775-notes-L002}{}{\textgreek{σελίδιον}.}
}
\DSource{0776}{327}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L001}{}{\ArabicRun{جري} — IV \textit{operari}; {\arabicfont ٧}\textsubscript{6-7} \ArabicRun{أَجْرَيْتُ في تصحيح ذلك ... على مذهب بطلميوس} « in his emendandis}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« operatus sum iuxta Ptolemaei rationem »; {\arabicfont ١٠٢}\textsubscript{3} \ArabicRun{وأَجْرِ فيه على الرسم ... من الطرح} « et in}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« hoc operare, iuxta praeceptum, subtractione utens »; {\arabicfont ١٠٢}\textsubscript{9-10} \ArabicRun{وأَجْرِ على الرَسم من إِلْقائها}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« et operare iuxta praeceptum in illis subducendis ». Sed {\arabicfont ١٠٢}\textsubscript{13} cum obiecto: \ArabicRun{ومَا بَقِيَ ...}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{فأَجْرِهِ على الرسم المتقدّم} « quod remanserit tracta iuxta praecedens praeceptum »; {\arabicfont ٨٧}\textsubscript{15} \ArabicRun{وأَجْرَى}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الحسابَ على ذلك}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L007}{}{\ArabicRun{جزأ} — II, apud al-Battānī et mathematicos non cum duplici accusativo, sed cum \ArabicRun{ب} partium}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}in quas data res dividitur construitur; cfr. \ArabicRun{قسم}. — \ArabicRun{جُزْءٌ} \textgreek{μοῖρα}, \textit{pars, gradus mathema-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ticus}, i. q. \ArabicRun{درجة}; {\arabicfont ٨}\textsubscript{19-20} (ubi definitur), ac persaepe. — \ArabicRun{أَجْزَاء العلوم} v. t. I, p. \textsc{xi}, n. 8.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L010}{10}{\ArabicRun{جسد} — III, n. act. \ArabicRun{مُجَاسَدَةٌ}, \textgreek{σωματικῶς συνάπτειν, ἡ τῶν σωμάτων συναφή}, \textit{corporaliter con-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{gredi, congressus corporeus} siderum, i. e. coniunctio quae fit non solum eodem gradu}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}longitudinis, sed et eodem latitudinis {\arabicfont ١٩٤}\textsubscript{21, 22}, {\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{15}. Definitio apud \Name{at–Tahānawī}, \textit{Di-}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ctionary}, ed. Sprenger, p. 196 et 1386, ed. Constant. 216 \ArabicRun{(مقارنة الكوكب بعقدة القمر).} haud}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}recta. — \ArabicRun{بروج ذوات الجَسَدَيْن} \textgreek{ζώδια δίσωμα}, \textit{signa zodiaci bicorpora} seu \textit{communia},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}i. e. Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces, t. I, p. \textsc{xxi}, n. 2 (ex \textit{Tetrabibli} versione).}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L016}{}{\ArabicRun{مُجَسَّمٌ} \textit{solidum, corpus geometricum}, {\arabicfont ٩٢}\textsubscript{10}, {\arabicfont ٩٣}\textsubscript{19}; cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I, 195,\textit{a}, ex Prol. et Bc.; \Name{Lane}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}s. v. \ArabicRun{جَسَّمَ}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{المِجْلَدُ} (?) \textgreek{π}\textsuperscript{6} \textit{Orionis} {\arabicfont ٢٦٧} (cfr. II, 169 n.).}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L019}{}{\ArabicRun{اِنْجلَاءٌ} \textgreek{ἀναπλήρωσις}, \textit{emersio} ex umbra, in eclipsibus, {\arabicfont ١٤٦} etc.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L020}{20}{\ArabicRun{المِجْمَرَةُ} \textgreek{τὸ Θυμιατήριον}, constellatio \textit{Arae}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٣}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L021}{}{\ArabicRun{الٱجْتِمَاعَاتُ} \textgreek{σύνοδοι}, \textit{coniunctiones} Solis et Lunae, persaepe; cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I, 217,\textit{b} e serioribus}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}scriptoribus.}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L023}{}{\ArabicRun{جنب} — \ArabicRun{جَنْبُ الدُّبّ الصغير} \textgreek{β} \textit{Ursae minoris}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٦}. — \ArabicRun{جَنْب فرساوس} \textgreek{α} \textit{Persei}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٦}.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L024}{}{\ArabicRun{جنح} — \ArabicRun{جَنَاح الفَرَس} \textgreek{α} \textit{Pegasi} {\arabicfont ٢٧٧}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L025}{25}{\ArabicRun{الجَوْزَهَرُ} in titulo cap. XXXVII, p. {\arabicfont ١١٣}, sed fortasse vox interpolata ut I, p. \textsc{xliii} monui. De vo-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cis sensu v. I, 250.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L027}{}{\ArabicRun{جُوِي رَاسْت} \textit{ascensio recta}, loco spurio {\arabicfont ٢٢٤}\textsubscript{11, 12}. De hac voce v. I, 325–26.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L028}{}{\ArabicRun{جَيْبٌ} \textit{sinus} trigonometricus, vox Albatenio ignota, v. I, 155–156. — \ArabicRun{جَيْب النهار} \textit{sinus ver-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{sus arcus semidiurni}, apud \Name{Ḥabash} aliosque t. I, 190.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L030}{30}{\ArabicRun{حَدٌّ} pl. \ArabicRun{حُدُودٌ} unus ex quatuor \textit{terminis} (\textgreek{ὅροι}) parallaxium lunarium altitudinis, {\arabicfont ١١٦}, {\arabicfont ١١٨}, et II,}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}93, 94, 235–36. — \textgreek{Ὅροι}, \textit{termini} planetarum iuxta astrologos, in tabula spuria p. {\arabicfont ٢٤٤};}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cfr. II, 309 et \Name{Dozy}, I, 255,\textit{a} (ex Ibn Khaldūn). — \ArabicRun{الحدود الكُسُوفيّة} \textgreek{ὅροι ἐκλειπτικοί}, \textit{ter-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{mini ecliptici}, i. e. maximae distantiae longitudinales Lunae ab alterutra nodi lunaris}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}parte, intra quas eclipses Solis aut Lunae fieri possunt, {\arabicfont ١٤٦}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ١٥٧}\textsubscript{9} et II, 32, 88, 206–209.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L035}{35}{\ArabicRun{حرس} — \ArabicRun{حَارِسُ الشَّمَال} \textgreek{ὁ Βοώτης}, constellatio \textit{Bootis}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٧}. Versio alterius nominis Graeci (ab}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Arato}, \Name{Vettio Valente} aliisque, at non a \Name{Ptolemaeo} usurpati) \textgreek{Ἀρκτοφύλαξ}, quo}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L037}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tam Arcturus (\textgreek{α} Bootis) quam constellatio tota designatur. Apud \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī} constellationem}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L038}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}totam designat p. 64, Arcturum p. 66.}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0776-E16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L039}{}{\ArabicRun{حرف} — \ArabicRun{اِنْحِرَافٌ} \textgreek{λόξωσις}, \textit{obliquitas, obliquatio, reflexio}, in Venere et Mercurio, i. e. incli-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0776-body-L040}{40}{\hspace*{21.28pt}natio diametri epicycli puncta mediarum distantiarum 90° et 270° coniungentis, respectu}
\DSource{0777}{328}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}plani deferentis, {\arabicfont ١٧٤} pluries, {\arabicfont ١٧٥}\textsubscript{10}, et II, 141, 250 sqq. — \ArabicRun{انحراف فلك التدوير} \textgreek{ἡ ἀπὸ τοῦ}}
\par\vspace{2.49pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἀπογείου τοῦ ἐκκέντρου ἀποχὴ τοῦ ἐπικύκλου}, \textit{elongatio centri epicycli} Lunae \textit{ab apogeo}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{excentrici}, in ecliptica ad successionem signorum computata, quae semper aequatur du-}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}plae elongationi Lunae mediae a Sole medio; \ArabicRun{فلك التدوير المُنْحَرِف} \textit{epicyclus declivis, epi-}}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{cyclus} Lunae \textit{ab apogeo excentrici elongatus}, {\arabicfont ٧٨}\textsubscript{8, 9}, {\arabicfont ٧٩}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ٨٠}\textsubscript{6}, {\arabicfont ٨١}\textsubscript{3}, {\arabicfont ١١٧}\textsubscript{11, 12}, {\arabicfont ١١٨}\textsubscript{3}. Item}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}in quinque planetis, \textit{elongatio centri epicycli ab apogeo excentrici}, in ecliptica vel in}
\par\vspace{2.73pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}aequante ad successionem signorum computata, {\arabicfont ٩٥}\textsubscript{16}, {\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{17, 22}. — \ArabicRun{انحراف الظُّلْمة} \textgreek{πρόσνευσις}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἐπισκοτήσεως}, \textit{inclinatio obscuritatis} respectu eclipticae in eclipsibus, {\arabicfont ١٥١} pluries, {\arabicfont ١٦٦}. —}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{انحراف الانجلاء} \textgreek{πρόσνευσις τοῦ ἀναπληρουμένου}, \textit{inclinatio emersionis} (seu partis emergentis)}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}respectu eclipticae in eclipsibus, {\arabicfont ١٥١} pluries.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0777-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L011}{}{\ArabicRun{حصص} — \ArabicRun{حِصَّةٌ} 1) \textit{portio} quaecumque; — 2) quantitas ad quam alia proportionalis debet ex}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tertia data sumi, \textit{quantitas proportionalis}: {\arabicfont ١٩}\textsubscript{9} et {\arabicfont ١٧٣}\textsubscript{22} \ArabicRun{فخذ لها بحصَّتها من تفاضل} « ex}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« intervallo [numerorum] sume partem ad quantitatem eius proportionalem » (1); {\arabicfont ٦٧}\textsubscript{20-21}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{ومعرفة حصّة درجة منه} « et cognitio portionis eius (i. e. portionis ex aequatione centri So-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}lis) [respondens] gradui [motus medii] »; {\arabicfont ٧٣}\textsubscript{9} \ArabicRun{فهو حصّة تلك الاجزاء التي للحاصّة التي}}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{عملْتَ بها لأيّ الكواكب قصدت من اختلاف حركته وهو تعديل الكوكب} « et hoc erit portio}
\par\vspace{2.41pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« inaequalitatis motus, seu aequationis planetae, pertinens ad gradus anomaliae quibus}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« usus es, pro quocumque planeta quem volueris »; {\arabicfont ٧٥}\textsubscript{16-17} \ArabicRun{وأخذنا حصّة كلّ جزء من اجزاء}}
\par\vspace{2.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{البروج من مقدار اختلاف الايّام بلياليها} « et ex quantitate inaequalitatis nychthemerōn sum-}
\par\vspace{2.59pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« psimus portionem respondentem singulis gradibus eclipticae »; item {\arabicfont ١٤٤}\textsubscript{15} et {\arabicfont ١٥٠}\textsubscript{12, 17}. Inde}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الحِصَص} \textit{partes proportionales} sexagesimae, quarum artificio tabulae simpliciores fiunt,}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἑξηκοστά}, {\arabicfont ١٠٧}\textsubscript{10}, {\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{5}, {\arabicfont ١٢١}\textsubscript{4}, {\arabicfont ١٣١}\textsubscript{16}, {\arabicfont ١٤٦}\textsubscript{20}, ac saepe in tabularum inscriptionibus. — 3) \textit{argu-}}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{mentum}, quantitas per quam alia, praesertim ex tabulis, requiritur; {\arabicfont ١٩}\textsubscript{16} \ArabicRun{اذا كانت حصّة}}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الميل من \AbjadZero{} الى قف فالميل شماليّ} « cum fuerit argumentum declinationis [eclipticae] a 0°}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« ad 180°, erit declinatio borealis » i. e. puncta eclipticae, quorum longitudo a 0° ad 180°,}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}declinationem habent borealem; {\arabicfont ١٩}\textsubscript{6-7} \ArabicRun{فخذ من اوّل الحمل الى درجة الشمس او غيرها ممّا تريد}}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{ان تعرف ميله فما كان فهو حصّة الميل} « intervallum ab Arietis initio ad gradum Solis vel}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« alium gradum, cuius declinationem scire cupis, sume; id erit argumentum declinatio-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« nis » etc. — 4) quoniam anomalia est argumentum per quod in tabulis aequatio centri}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}requiritur, apud nonnullos designat \textit{anomaliam} Solis, Lunae, planetarum; cfr. II, 226 et}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Ḥabash} f. 19,r. (\ArabicRun{فهو خاصة الشمس ويسمى الحصة ايضًا}), 24,r.–v., 26,r.; \textit{Mafātīḥ}, 223:}
\par\vspace{2.06pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الخاصّة هو سير الكوكب نفسه في فلك التدوير ويسمَّى الحِصّة وهو بالفارسيّة الكَنْدَر}. — \ArabicRun{حصّة}}
\par\vspace{2.95pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{السَّمْت} \textit{argumentum azimuthi} apud nonnullos, i. q. sinus differentiae horizontis apud al-}
\par\vspace{2.35pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Battānī et Ibn Yūnus, v. I, 183. — \ArabicRun{حِصَّة العَرْض} \textit{argumentum latitudinis}, i. e. elongatio}
\par\vspace{2.48pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}a dato puncto excentrici per quam in tabulis latitudo Lunae quaeritur, {\arabicfont ١١٣}\textsubscript{16, 17, 19}, {\arabicfont ١١٤}\textsubscript{1, 2}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Cfr. \Name{at–Tahānawī} ed. Sprenger, p. 308, ed. Constant., p. 338. — \ArabicRun{حَاصَّة} \textit{anomalia}, i. e.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-body-L037}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}arcus epicycli quem ab apogeo ipsius epicycli Sol, Luna, planetae iam perambulaverunt (2);}
\par\vspace{6.34pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5.35pt}

\par\vspace{0.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-notes_left-L003}{}{\textgreek{ἀκουστέον δὲ καϑόλου} ... \textgreek{ἀνωμαλίαν δὲ τὴν} [\textgreek{κίνησιν}]}

\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\vspace*{0.54pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-notes_right-L001}{}{\textgreek{τοῦ ἀστέρος περὶ τὸν ἐπίκυκλον} « generaliter intelli-}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-notes_right-L002}{}{« gendum est anomaliae nomine motum planetae in}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0777-notes_right-L003}{}{« circumferentia epicycli ».}

\DSource{0778}{329}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}de Sole, {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{18} (ubi definitio), {\arabicfont ١٠٧}\textsubscript{10, 13}, {\arabicfont ١١١}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ١٢١}\textsubscript{4}, {\arabicfont ١٤١}\textsubscript{16}; de Luna et planetis {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٧٣}\textsubscript{9},}
\par\vspace{2.36pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٧٨} etc. In planetis intelligitur arcus ab apogeo medio epicycli computatus ab occidente}
\par\vspace{2.38pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}in orientem versus; si ab apogeo vero epicycli numeratur est \ArabicRun{الخاصّة المعدَّلة} \textit{ano-}}
\par\vspace{2.54pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{malia aequata} seu \textit{vera}, {\arabicfont ٩٧}–{\arabicfont ٩٨}, {\arabicfont ١٧٢} etc. Proprie \ArabicRun{الحركة الخاصّة} « motus [peculiaris in epi-}
\par\vspace{2.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« cyclo] contingens [Soli, Lunae, planetae dato] » (1); \Name{Plato Tiburtinus} semper}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{portionem} vertit. Plerumque ceteri astronomi \ArabicRun{الخاصّة} « [motum] proprium » eam vocant,}
\par\vspace{2.14pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}unde \textgreek{τὸ ἴδιον, ἡ ἰδία κίνησις} in versione \Name{Sampsucharis}, in tabulis \Name{Chrysococcae},}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}in tertia parte \Name{Theodoris Meliteniotae}; cfr. {\arabicfont ٦٨}\textsubscript{7} \ArabicRun{هي حركة الاختلاف الخاصّ للكوكب};}
\par\vspace{2.22pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٧٨}\textsubscript{5} \ArabicRun{ان قوس ط ح اختلاف حركة القمر في مسيره الخاصّ له في فلك التدوير}; {\arabicfont ٨٣}\textsubscript{3} \ArabicRun{مسيره الخاصّ له}}
\par\vspace{2.43pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(de Luna); {\arabicfont ٨٥}\textsubscript{6} \ArabicRun{مسيره الخاصّ في فلك التدوير} (de Luna).}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0778-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L011}{}{\ArabicRun{حصر} — VII, \textit{computari, numerari, numero comprehendi}; {\arabicfont ١٨٣}\textsubscript{6} \ArabicRun{وشيئًا يسيرًا لا ينحصر}; {\arabicfont ١٨٤}\textsubscript{5}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{غير شيء يسير لا ينحصر}; \Name{Freytag} ex Golii Marg. (2). Cfr. \ArabicRun{حصر} apud \Name{Dozy}, I, 294,\textit{b};}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Friedlaender}, 25,\textit{a}; \textit{Gloss. Tanbīh}, XVI.}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0778-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L014}{}{\ArabicRun{حطّ} (I et VII) v. \ArabicRun{رفع}.}
\par\vspace{2.31pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0778-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L015}{15}{\ArabicRun{حكم} — IV, \textit{accurate deprehendere, definire}, {\arabicfont ١٢٣}\textsubscript{15}, {\arabicfont ١٤٨}\textsubscript{11, 24}; cfr. \Name{Lane} et \Name{Dozy}, I, 309,\textit{b}.}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— \ArabicRun{مُحْكَمٌ} \textit{praecise definitus, correctus, exacte constitutus}, oppos. \ArabicRun{مُطْلَقٌ}, {\arabicfont ١٤٨}\textsubscript{20, 23}, {\arabicfont ٢٢٦}\textsubscript{6}; eius}
\par\vspace{2.30pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}synonymum \ArabicRun{مُعَدَّلٌ} {\arabicfont ١٥٩}\textsubscript{20, 21}, {\arabicfont ١٦٠}\textsubscript{1, 8}, {\arabicfont ١٦٧} pluries. — \ArabicRun{أَحْكَمُ} comparativus a \ArabicRun{مُحْكَمٌ}: {\arabicfont ١٤٥}\textsubscript{2} \ArabicRun{انّ ذلك}}
\par\vspace{2.35pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{هو اصحّ واحكم} « id certius et verius cognoscitur »; {\arabicfont ١٧٨}\textsubscript{16} \ArabicRun{اصحّ واحكم} « melius et certius ».}
\par\vspace{2.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Cfr. \textit{Gloss. Tabari}, p. \textsc{cxcix}.}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0778-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L020}{20}{\ArabicRun{حَلْقَةٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{حَلَقٌ} \textit{armilla, circulus metallicus} in instrumentis astronomicis, {\arabicfont ٢١١}–{\arabicfont ٢١٤}; unde \ArabicRun{ذات الحَلَق}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἡ κρικωτὴ σφαῖρα}, \textit{sphaera armillaris}, {\arabicfont ١٨٧}\textsubscript{18} (cfr. \Name{Dozy} ex Prol.). — \textit{Armilla suspen-}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{soria} astrolabii, unde apud nostrates \textit{alhantica, alphantia, abalhantica}, {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{17}; cfr. \Name{Dorn},}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Drei Instr.}, 27, 141; \Name{Dozy}, I, 317,\textit{a}; et quae s. v. \ArabicRun{عروة} expono.}
\par\vspace{2.34pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L024}{}{\ArabicRun{الحِمَارَانِ} \textgreek{οἱ ὄνοι}, \textit{Aselli}, \textgreek{γ} et \textgreek{δ} \textit{Cancri}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٧}; cfr. \Name{aṣ–Ṣūfī}, 151. Perperam \Name{Lane} \textgreek{α} et \textgreek{δ}.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0778-body-L025}{25}{\ArabicRun{الحامل} v. \ArabicRun{فلك}.}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0778-E07}{}

\par\vspace{2.38pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0779-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{diurnorum}, {\arabicfont ٤٩}\textsubscript{18, 19, 21}, {\arabicfont ٥٠}\textsubscript{3, 4}. — \ArabicRun{اختلاف مَجَاز الكوكب في وَسَط السماء} \textit{differentia inter}}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0779-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{lae}, {\arabicfont ٤٨} pluries (cfr. I, 194). — \ArabicRun{اختلاف المَنْظَر} \textgreek{παράλλαξις}, \textit{parallaxis}, quae fit \ArabicRun{في الطول} \textgreek{κατὰ}}

\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0779-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ρυφὴν σημείου καὶ τοῦ κέντρου τῆς σελήνης γραφομένου μεγίστου κύκλου}, \textit{altitudinis}, {\arabicfont ١١٤}–{\arabicfont ١٢٧} et}
\par\vspace{2.35pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0780-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}143, n. 3: « Le scorpion est le domicile de Mars (Arès), qui y a son Sosie, Antarès, \textgreek{τῷ}}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0785-body-L039}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}constellatio \textit{Lupi}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٢}; \Name{Lane} ex al-Qazwīnī. — \ArabicRun{سَبُعُ البَحْر} \textgreek{τὸ Κῆτος}, constellatio \textit{Ceti}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٦}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0785-E14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0785-body-L040}{40}{\ArabicRun{سبق} — \ArabicRun{سَبْقُ القَمَر [للشمس]} \textit{motus synodicus vel relativus Lunae}, {\arabicfont ١٣٠}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ١٤٥} etc. — \ArabicRun{سَبْق}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0785-body-L041}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{القمر المُخْتَلِف للشمس في الساعة} \textit{motus inaequalis relativus Lunae in hora}, {\arabicfont ١٤١}\textsubscript{21}.}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E01}{}

\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L002}{}{\ArabicRun{[سَادِسَة]} pl. \ArabicRun{سَوَادِسُ} \textit{sexta pars sexagesima, minutum sextum}, {\arabicfont ١٠}, {\arabicfont ١١} ter, {\arabicfont ١٢}. — \ArabicRun{تَسْدِيسٌ} \textgreek{ἑξά}-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{γωνον}, \textit{sextilis aspectus}, i. e. sexta circuli eclipticae pars, intervallum 60° inter longitu-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}dines duorum siderum. {\arabicfont ١٩٤}–{\arabicfont ١٩٧} (cfr. \Name{Dozy}, I, 642 ex Prol., Bc, 1001 N).}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L005}{5}{\ArabicRun{سَرِيرٌ} \textgreek{κατάστρωμα}, \textit{tabulatus navis}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٨}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٩} (cfr. II, 275 in vers. Hispan. « lecho »).}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L006}{}{\ArabicRun{سطر} — \ArabicRun{سَطْرُ العَدَد} pl. \ArabicRun{سُطُور العَدَد} vel \ArabicRun{سُطُور الأَعْدَاد} \textgreek{τοῦ ἀριϑμοῦ σελίδιον}, \textit{columna tabulae mathe-}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{thematicae exhibens argumentum}, {\arabicfont ١٥}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ١٦} pluries, {\arabicfont ١٨}–{\arabicfont ١٩}, {\arabicfont ٣٢}\textsubscript{23}, {\arabicfont ٣٣}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ٢١٨}\textsubscript{14} ac per-}
\par\vspace{2.10pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}saepe; item in titulis tabularum fere omnium (1). Columnae quibus functiones exhibentur}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}appellantur \ArabicRun{جداول}. — \ArabicRun{سُطُور العَدَد المربَّع} \textit{columna quadruplicis argumenti}, t. II, p. 57-}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}58. — \ArabicRun{سَطْر العَدَد المُشْتَرِك} \textgreek{ἀριϑμοὶ κοινοί}, \textit{columna exhibens argumentum commune plu-}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ribus tabulis}, {\arabicfont ٤٠}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٤١}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ٤٣}\textsubscript{1}, et t. II, 61-64, 68-71.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L012}{}{\ArabicRun{سعد} — \ArabicRun{سَعْدُ بُلَعَ} vigesima tertia statio lunaris complectens \textgreek{ν} vel \textit{Fl. 13} (iuxta Schjellerup}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Fl. 7}), \textgreek{μ} \textit{et} \textgreek{ε} \textit{Aquarii}, {\arabicfont ١٨٩}\textsubscript{6}; cfr. hoc t. II addit. ad I, 125. — \ArabicRun{سَعْدُ السُّعُودِ} vigesima quarta}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}statio lunaris complectens \textgreek{β} \textit{et} \textgreek{ξ} \textit{Aquarii ac c'} (Schjell. \textit{c}) \textit{Capricorni}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L015}{15}{\ArabicRun{السَّفِينَةُ} \textgreek{ἡ Ἀργώ}, constellatio \textit{Navis} vel \textit{Argūs}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٨}; \Name{Lane} ex al-Qazwīnī.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L016}{}{\ArabicRun{سُقُوطٌ} \textgreek{ἔμπτωσις, ἀρχὴ τῆς ἐκλίψεως}, \textit{immersio}, una ex eclipsium phasibus, {\arabicfont ١٤٧} sqq.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L017}{}{\ArabicRun{السَّاقِي} \textgreek{ὁ Ὑδροχόος}, constellatio \textit{Aquarii}, in catalogo fixarum ex Almagesto {\arabicfont ٢٦٣}; sed alibi sem-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}per \ArabicRun{الدَّلْو}.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L019}{}{\ArabicRun{سلس} — {\arabicfont ٣٤}\textsubscript{17} \ArabicRun{مَوْضع سَلِسُ الوَجْهِ} locus \textit{planae} superficiei (Plato Tiburt.: « planaeque faciei »);}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٢٠٣}\textsubscript{10-11} \ArabicRun{صفيحة نُحَاس مستوية السطح سَلِسَة الوجه} « aequalis et \textit{laevigatae} superficiei »;}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٢١٠}\textsubscript{5} \ArabicRun{سلسة السطح} « \textit{expolitae} superficiei »; {\arabicfont ٢١٥}\textsubscript{8} \ArabicRun{ويكون وجه اللِّبْنَة سَلِسًا} « lateris superfi-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« cies sit \textit{expolita} ».}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L023}{}{\ArabicRun{سمت} \textit{directio} quaelibet, {\arabicfont ٩٢}\textsubscript{21}, {\arabicfont ٩٣}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٩٦}\textsubscript{17}, {\arabicfont ١٣٦}\textsubscript{19}, {\arabicfont ١٨٧}\textsubscript{13}; unde \ArabicRun{سَمْت الرَّأْس} vel \ArabicRun{سَمْت الرُّؤُس} « di-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}rectio capitis », \textgreek{ἡ κορυφή, τὸ κατὰ κορυφὴν σημεῖον}, « zenith capitis » vel « zenith capitum »}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}apud Platonem Tiburtinum, « el zonte (el zont) de la cabeça » in versione Hispanica (2). —}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{سَمْت ... من دائرة الأُفْق} « directio ... in circulo horizontis » in gradibus circuli computata,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{azimuth} (« zenith » (3) apud Platonem Tiburtinum), cuius exempla innumera. Notandum}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}quod Arabes veteres \textit{azimuth} a puncto E vel W horizontis numerant, nos a puncto S}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vel N; qua re azimuth punctorum eclipticae apud Arabes ab \textit{amplitudine} non differt.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Solis p. {\arabicfont ٢٩}–{\arabicfont ٣٠} et {\arabicfont ٣٤} al-Battānī \ArabicRun{من دائرة الافق} omittit; ergo \ArabicRun{سمت المَطْلَع} \textit{amplitudo ortiva},}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{سمت المَغِيب} \textit{amplitudo occidua}, \ArabicRun{سمت الجَنُوب} \textit{azimuth} seu \textit{directio meridiei}. — \ArabicRun{سمت}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{القِبْلَة} \textit{azimuth qiblae, directio qiblae}, i. e. angulus interceptus arcu circuli magni per-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}pendicularis ad meridianum datae urbis et arcu circuli magni datam urbem cum Mekkah}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}coniungentis, {\arabicfont ٢٠٦}–{\arabicfont ٢٠٨}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0786-E11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L035}{35}{\ArabicRun{سَمِعَ} a, cum \ArabicRun{ل}, \textgreek{ἀκούειν}, \textit{audire}, de puncto eclipticae quod ab aequinoctiali puncto retrorsum}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0786-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(i. e. in hemisphaerio australi) tanto distat quanto antrorsum aliud punctum (\ArabicRun{آمِرٌ}), {\arabicfont ١٩٥}\textsubscript{19},}
