\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{1}. — \ArabicRun{جُزْءٌ سَامِعٌ} \textgreek{ἀκοῦον τμῆμα}, \textit{gradus audiens, obediens}, apud astrologos, i. q. \ArabicRun{مُنْخَفِضٌ},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{مُطِيعٌ}, {\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{6}.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L003}{}{\ArabicRun{سَمْكٌ} \textit{crassitudo} armillae, scilicet spatium inter superficiem concavam interiorem et convexam}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}superiorem, {\arabicfont ٢١١}\textsubscript{12, 16}, {\arabicfont ٢١٢}\textsubscript{7, 14, 16, 21}, {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{1, 2, 12}; ita geometrae designant \textit{profunditatem} (\textgreek{τὸ}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{βάϑος}) corporum. Cfr. \Name{Lane}: « in the present day, it signifies the \textit{extent} of anything \textit{from}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« \textit{top to bottom}; its \textit{height, depth} und \textit{thickness} ». — \ArabicRun{السَّمَكَتَانِ} \textgreek{οἱ Ἰχϑύες}, constellatio}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Piscium}, in catalogo fixarum ex Almagesto, {\arabicfont ٢٦٥}; sed alibi semper \ArabicRun{الحوت}. Cfr. \Name{Lane}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L008}{}{\ArabicRun{سَمَّى} cum acc. et \ArabicRun{من}, \textit{quantitatem minorem per alteram maiorem dividere}, iuxta Arabes}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}occidentales, loco spurio {\arabicfont ٢٢٥}\textsubscript{2}: \ArabicRun{وسَمِّ ذلك من نصف قوس الليل وخذ ذلك الاسم من فضلة}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الفضلتيْن}. V. quae dixi t. I, p. \textsc{lxxix-lxxx} et 326; praeterea \Name{Muḥ}. b. \Name{Aḥmad} b. Muḥ.}
\par\vspace{1.38pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{aṣ–Ṣabbāgh} (m. post 1060 heg.), \textit{Silk farā’id al-yawāqīt}, Fez 1319, p. 25: \ArabicRun{فصل في}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{التسمية وهي من انواع القسمة لا انها مختصّة بقسمة قليل على كثير فلا يخرج فيها عدد}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{صحيح ابدًا ... وهي قِسْمانِ تسمية صحيح من صحيح وتسمية كسر او مع صحيح من صحيح او مع كسر}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Cfr. infra s. v. \ArabicRun{نسبة}. — \ArabicRun{اِسْمٌ} \textit{ratio} mathematica (i. q. \ArabicRun{نسبة}), loco spurio nuper adducto; cfr. I,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}326 et s. v. \ArabicRun{نسبة}. — \ArabicRun{المُسَمَّى} fractionis \textit{numerator}, I, 326; \ArabicRun{المسمَّى منه} \textit{denominator}, I, 326.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L016}{}{\ArabicRun{السُّنْبُلَةُ} \textit{a}) \textgreek{ἡ Πάρϑενος}, constellatio \textit{Virginis}, persaepe. \textit{b}) \textgreek{ὁ Στάχυς}, \textit{Spica}, \textgreek{α} \textit{Virginis}, i. q.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{السِّمَاك الأَعْزَل}, in catal. fixarum {\arabicfont ٢٥٩}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{سنو} — \ArabicRun{سنون مَبْسُوطة} v. \ArabicRun{بسط}. — \ArabicRun{سنون مَجْمُوعَة} \textit{anni collecti} ab aera, annorum summa ab}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}epocha aerae computata, non annorum spatium a quolibet temporis momento numeratum,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}v. II, 199 ac persaepe. — \ArabicRun{سنون تَامَّة} \textit{anni tropici}, {\arabicfont ١٩٢}, {\arabicfont ٢٢٣} ac tabula II, 187 (cfr. II, 295).}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— Valde incertum {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{22} \ArabicRun{ودور الفلك هناك دَوْلابيّ وهو السنوي}. Verti I, 14: « et sphaera ad}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« similitudinem rotae aquariae vel rotae putei », respiciens \ArabicRun{سَانِيَة} i. q. \ArabicRun{دَوْلاب} (1). Sed tunc}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}legendum \ArabicRun{السّنَوِيّ} = \ArabicRun{السَّانَوِيّ} videtur. Est rotatio \textit{verticalis} circa axem horizontalem.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L024}{}{\ArabicRun{سهم} — \ArabicRun{سَهْم دائرة الشمس} \textit{sagitta disci solaris}, i. e. sagitta partis disci eclipsatae, {\arabicfont ١٦٤}\textsubscript{21, 22};}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{سهم دائرة القمر} \textit{sagitta disci lunaris}, {\arabicfont ١٦٥}\textsubscript{2}; \ArabicRun{سهم الظِّلّ} \textit{sagitta umbrae} in eclipsibus,}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ١٥٠} pluries. V. I, 276. — \ArabicRun{سَهْمُ السَّعَادَة} \textgreek{ὁ κλῆρος τῆς Τύχης}, \textit{pars Fortunae}, v. I, 314, n. 1,}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}ubi veram definitionem dedi, qualis e placitis Ptolemaei (2) maximaeque partis astrologo-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}rum Arabicorum colligitur. Genituras diurnas a nocturnis non distinguunt. Nec a Ptole-}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}maica ratione differunt praecepta \Name{Nechepsonis} et \Name{Petosiris}, quamquam duplici}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}modo, diurno et nocturno, Partem Fortunae inveniendam statuunt. Die enim intervallum}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}a \SunSym{} ad \MoonSym{}, iuxta ordinem signorum sumptum, addunt ascendenti « in \textit{sinistram} partem »}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(i. e. iuxta ordinem signorum); nocte intervallum a \MoonSym{} ad \SunSym{}, i. e. complementum in-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tervalli diurni ad 360°, addunt ascendenti « in \textit{dexteram} partem ». Quod alterutra via}
\par\vspace{10.37pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{10.49pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Plato} \ArabicRun{وهو السنوي} omittit; quae ante-}
\par\vspace{0.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L002}{}{cedunt vertit: « coelique circumrotatio tornatoris}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L003}{}{« instrumenti formam obtinet ».}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0787-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L004}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Tetrab.}, III, 10 (= Francof. III, 12): « Pars}
\par\vspace{0.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L005}{}{« Fortunae est quod omni tempore (\textgreek{πάντοτε}), die et no-}
\par\vspace{0.61pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L006}{}{« cte, colligitur ex numero graduum intervalli a \SunSym{} ad}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L007}{}{« \MoonSym{}; quem numerum ab ascendente proiciemus (\textgreek{ἀφαι}-}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_left-L008}{}{« \textgreek{ροῦντες ἀπὸ τοῦ ὡροσκόπου}) in consequentia signo-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L001}{}{« rum (i. e. in ortum versus computantes). Quo in-}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L002}{}{« ciderit numerus, illum zodiaci gradum et locum}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L003}{}{« obtineri a Parte Fortunae dicemus; ut quam ra-}
\par\vspace{0.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L004}{}{« tionem (\textgreek{λόγον}) quamque configurationem (\textgreek{σχηματι}-}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L005}{}{« \textgreek{σμόν}, i. e. distantiam angularem) Sol ad ascendens}
\par\vspace{0.51pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L006}{}{« (\textgreek{πρὸς τὸν ἀνατολικὸν ὁρίζοντα}) habet, eandem Luna}
\par\vspace{0.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L007}{}{« habeat ad Partem Fortunae, quae ita quasi ascen-}
\par\vspace{0.42pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0787-notes_right-L008}{}{« dens lunare (\textgreek{ὥσπερ σεληνιακὸς ὡροσκόπος}) fiet ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0788}{339}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}colligitur semper est unum et idem, ac rationi Ptolemaicae congruit. Tamen \Name{Vettius}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Valens}, quem \Name{Firmicus} interdum sequitur, additionem in sinistram partem semper fieri}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}iubet; qua re diversus fit locus Partis Fortunae diurnus a nocturno (1). Definitio \Name{at-Ta-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{hānawī}, \textit{Dictionary}, p. 698 (ed. Constant. 772), nisi mutila est, Vettii Valentis senten-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tiam sequitur: « \textit{Die} sumunt [intervallum longitudinis] a \SunSym{} ad gradum \MoonSym{}, eique gradum}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« ascendentis addunt; postea ab ascendente per tricenos gradus summam proiciunt, ac quo}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« subtractio pervenerit ibi erit locus Partis Fortunae. \textit{Noctu} [intervallum] a \MoonSym{} ad gradum}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« \SunSym{} sumunt, eique gradum ascendentis addunt ». Sequitur exemplum diurnum: Sit ascen-}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}dens in 10° Arietis, \SunSym{} 20° Leonis, \MoonSym{} 15° Librae; tunc \SunSym{} — \MoonSym{} = 55°, quibus addendi 10°}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}ascendentis. Fiunt 65°. Datis 30° Arieti, Tauro 30°, relinquuntur 5° Geminis; erit igitur}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}locus Partis Fortunae in 5° Geminorum.}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0788-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L012}{}{\ArabicRun{سوع} — \ArabicRun{سَاعَةٌ مُسْتَوِيَةٌ} ({\arabicfont ٢١٤}\textsubscript{12}) vel \ArabicRun{سَاعَةٌ مُعْتَدِلَةٌ} (persaepe) vel \ArabicRun{سَاعَةُ الٱعْتِدَالِ} (saepe), \textgreek{ἡ ὁμαλὴ} vel}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἴση} vel \textgreek{ἰσημερινὴ ὥρα}, \textit{hora aequinoctialis}, vigesimaquarta pars rotationis diurnae, com-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}plectens 15° aequatoris. — \ArabicRun{سَاعَةٌ مُعْوَجَّةٌ} ({\arabicfont ٤٢}\textsubscript{15}), \ArabicRun{سَاعَةٌ زَمَانِيَّةٌ} (persaepe), \textgreek{ἡ ἀνώμαλος} vel \textgreek{ἄνισος}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vel \textgreek{καιρικὴ ὥρα}, \textit{hora temporalis}, duodecima pars arcus diurni aut arcus nocturni Solis.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Definitio \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh}, p. 225, l. 18-21, bona; sed omnino falsa quae in \textit{Gloss.}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Tanbīh}, p. \textsc{xxxviii}, s. v. \ArabicRun{عوج} dicuntur.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0788-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{سوى} — \ArabicRun{دَرَجُ السَّوَاء} \textit{gradus eclipticae}, ita vocati cum respectu correspondentium ascensionum}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}rectarum aut obliquarum considerantur, \textit{gradus aequales} apud nostrates aevi medii, \textit{gra-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{dos eguales} in \textit{Libros del saber de astronomia}, \texthebrew{מעלות השוה} in versione Hebraica libri}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{az-Zarqālī} (2), {\arabicfont ٤١}\textsubscript{7} (\ArabicRun{التي هي درج البروج}) et in titulis tabularum II, 69-71. Vox iam apud}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Muḥ}. b. \Name{‘Omar} b. \Name{al-Farrukhān} (3); frequens apud \Name{Ḥabash}; praeterea v. \Name{Ibn}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Hibintà}, f. 33,v., 34,r.; \Name{al-Bīrūnī} (apud Nicoll, \textit{Catal. Bodlei.}, II, p. 538,\textit{a}); \Name{Barhe-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{braei}, \textit{Astr. Syr.}, 163 text., 144 vers., aliaque exempla a me t. I, p. 28, n. 3 adducta.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— \ArabicRun{خَطُّ الٱسْتِوَاء} \textgreek{ὁ ἰσημερινὸς κύκλος}, \textit{linea aequinoctialis, aequator terrestris}, at non cae-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}lestis (cfr. perspicuam definitionem \Name{al-Ǵaghmīnī}, 232), {\arabicfont ٢٠}\textsubscript{7}, {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{11} ac saepe. Ex Arabico}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}nomine \textgreek{ἡ ὀρϑὴ γραμμή} in tabulis geographicis \Name{Chrysococcae}. — \ArabicRun{ساعة مُسْتَوِيَة} v. \ArabicRun{ساعة}.}
\par\vspace{1.99pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— \ArabicRun{ظِلّ مُسْتَوٍ} v. \ArabicRun{ظلّ}.}
\par\vspace{2.48pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0788-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L029}{}{\ArabicRun{تَسْيِيرٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{تَسْيِيرَاتٌ} \textgreek{ἡ ἄφεσις}, actio \textit{dirigendi} aphetam, \textit{directio astrologica} aphetae, {\arabicfont ٦١}\textsubscript{1}, {\arabicfont ١٩٦}\textsubscript{17},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٢٠٣}\textsubscript{1} et adpend. spuria {\arabicfont ٢٢٤}–{\arabicfont ٢٢٥}; v. quae dixi t. I, 325. Nec exactae nec sufficientes defini-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}tiones \textit{Mafātīḥ}, 230; \Name{at-Tahānawī}, ed. Sprenger, 285 (ed. Constant., 313, s. v. \ArabicRun{حدّ});}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Dozy}, I, 712 (ex Prol.). — \ArabicRun{الكَوَاكِبُ السَّيَّارَةُ} \textit{septem planetae}, i. e. \textit{Sol et Luna et quin-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{que planetae}, {\arabicfont ١٧٩}\textsubscript{13} (\ArabicRun{الكواكب الجارية وهي السيّارة المتحيّرة}), {\arabicfont ١٨٧}\textsubscript{14} (\ArabicRun{الكواكب الجارية وهي السبعة}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{السيّارة}). Si de 5 planetis proprie dictis solummodo agitur, dicitur \ArabicRun{الكواكب الخمسة المتحيّرة};}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0788-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cf. \Name{Mīram Čelebī} in notis ad \Name{Oloug Beg}, p. 102 text.: \ArabicRun{شمس وقمررا نيّرين ڭويند}}
\par\vspace{9.55pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{7.86pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{وپنج كوكب ديڭررا متحيّره وهر يك از سبعه سياره‌را فلكى است كه ...}; \Name{Dozy}, I, 712,\textit{b} (ex M);}
\par\vspace{2.43pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{at-Tahānawī}, \textit{Dictionary}, ed. Sprenger, 664, ed. Constant., 733. Ergo \Name{Lane} emen-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}dandus. In \textit{Almagesto} ac \textit{Tetrabiblo} \textgreek{οἱ πλανώμενοι ἀστέρες} semper sunt soli 5 planetae,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}excepto ultimo \textit{Tetrab.} capite (IV, 9 = Francof. IV, 11, de temporum partitione) quo \textgreek{ἑπτὰ}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{πλανώμενοι} occurrunt. — \ArabicRun{المَسِيرُ الأَوْسَطُ} \textit{a}) \textit{motus medius}, \textit{b}) \textit{longitudo media} Solis, Lu-}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}nae, planetarum; v. II, 203-204. Item \textgreek{ἡ μέση πάροδος}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0789-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L007}{}{\ArabicRun{الشُّجَاعُ} \textgreek{ὁ Ὕδρος}, constellatio \textit{Hydrae}, {\arabicfont ٢٧١} bis, {\arabicfont ٢٧٩}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0789-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L008}{}{\ArabicRun{الشَّرَطَانِ} \textit{prima stationum lunarium} complectens \textgreek{β} \textit{et} \textgreek{γ} \textit{Arietis}, {\arabicfont ١٨٨}\textsubscript{21}. Perperam \Name{Lane} \textgreek{α} et \textgreek{β}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0789-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L009}{}{\ArabicRun{[شَرَفٌ]} pl. \ArabicRun{أَشْرَافٌ} \textgreek{ὕψωμα}, \textit{exaltatio, altitudo astrologica} i. e. signum zodiaci vel zodiaci gradus}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}quo planeta maximam potestatem obtinet, in tabula spuria {\arabicfont ٢٤٤} bis; cfr. II, 313. Definitio}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}nimis brevis apud \Name{Dozy}, I, 749,\textit{a} (ex Prol.).}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0789-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L012}{}{\ArabicRun{تَشْرِيقٌ} \textit{ortus} planetarum aut fixarum \textit{quotiescumque cum situ Solis comparatur} (1), ut fa-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cile elicitur e locis quibus vox occurrit, \textgreek{ἀνατολὴ} seu \textgreek{φάσις ἡ πρὸς τὸν ἥλιον γινομένη}, {\arabicfont ١٧٦}\textsubscript{11}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(ubi \ArabicRun{الطلوع} definitur, sed de apparitione ex radiis Solis agitur), {\arabicfont ١٧٩}\textsubscript{17} (\ArabicRun{وقد يقال التشريق}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{اذا طلع الكوكب قبل الشمس} i. e. \textit{hortus heliacus}), et II, 142. Unde: \ArabicRun{تَشْرِيقُ الغَدَوَاتِ} \textit{cum},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Sole et stella in orientali plaga exstantibus, stella post Solem aut simul cum Sole}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{oriatur}, {\arabicfont ١٧٩}\textsubscript{14} (cfr. I, 284, nr. 1); in tabulis planetarum inferiorum II, 142 \ArabicRun{طلوع الغدوات}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{φάσις ἑῴας ἀνατολῆς}. \ArabicRun{وَسَطُ سَمَاء التَّشْرِيق} \textit{cum stella meridianum superiorem teneat per-}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{paulo post Solis ortum aut oriente Sole}, {\arabicfont ١٧٩}\textsubscript{18} (cfr. I, 284, nr. 2). \ArabicRun{جَنُوبِيُّ التَّشْرِيقِ} \textit{cum}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{stella ex oriente oriatur perpaulo ante aut perpaulo post Solem aut simul cum So-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{lis culminatione superiore vel inferiore}, {\arabicfont ١٨٠}\textsubscript{2} (cfr. I, 284, nr. 4). — \ArabicRun{مَشْرِقُ الٱعْتِدَالِ} \textgreek{ἰση}-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{μερινή ἀνατολή}, \textit{punctum E horizontis, plaga orientalis} {\arabicfont ٢٠}\textsubscript{18} (i. q. \ArabicRun{مَطْلَع الاعتدال} {\arabicfont ٢٤٣}\textsubscript{2}). —}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{مَشَارِقُ شَتَوِيَّةٌ} \textgreek{χειμερινὴ ἀνατολή}, \textit{hortus hiemales}, et \ArabicRun{مَشَارِقُ صَيْفِيَّةٌ} \textgreek{ϑερινὴ ἀνατολή}, \textit{ortus}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0789-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{aestivi}, {\arabicfont ٢٩}\textsubscript{16-17}, {\arabicfont ٢١٤}\textsubscript{15}, {\arabicfont ٢٤٣}.}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0789-E05}{}

\DSource{0792}{343}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}potest \textit{dualis} confici cum ascensiones rectae obliquis opponantur; itaque \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 150,v.,}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}l. 5, f. 166,r. ult., et f. 166,v., l. 1: \ArabicRun{الفضل بين المَطَالِعَيْنِ}. Monendum est Arabes vocem}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{مطالع} usurpare de \textit{punctis eclipticae} ac de \textit{Sole} tantum, non de quocumque sidere aut}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}puncto sphaerae caelestis ut nostrates faciunt. Duplex ascensionum genus: \textit{a}) \ArabicRun{المَطَالِعُ}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{في الفَلَك المُسْتَقِيم} (vel \ArabicRun{مطالع البروج}), vel \ArabicRun{مطالع الفلك المستقيم} \textgreek{ἀναφορὰ ἐπ’ ὀρϑῆς τῆς σφαίρας},}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ascensio recta} Solis vel puncti eclipticae, persaepe ({\arabicfont ٢٠}\textsubscript{7}: \ArabicRun{في موضع خَطّ الٱسْتِوَاء}). Si de pun-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}ctis aut sideribus \textit{extra eclipticam} (1) agitur: \ArabicRun{مطالع الاجزاء التي تتوسّط السماء مع الكوكب}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« ascensiones graduum [zodiaci] qui simul cum stella Caelum mediant » seu culminant}
\par\vspace{1.92pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}seu per meridianum superiorem transeunt. \textit{b}) \ArabicRun{الإِقْلِيم} \ArabicRun{في} (vel \ArabicRun{مطالع البروج}) \ArabicRun{المطالع}, vel \ArabicRun{المطالع}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{في البلد}, vel \ArabicRun{مطالع الاقليم} \textgreek{ἡ ἐπὶ τῆς ἐγκεκλιμένης σφαίρας ἀναφορά, ἀναφορὰ τοῦ κλίματος} vel}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἐν τῷ κλίματι}, \textit{ascensio obliqua} puncti eclipticae (aut Solis), {\arabicfont ٣٩} ac persaepe. Pro sideribus}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}vel punctis \textit{extra eclipticam}: \ArabicRun{مطالع الاجزاء التي يطلع معها الكوكب في البلد} « ascensiones}
\par\vspace{2.10pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« graduum [eclipticae] cum quibus stella in urbe data ascendit [ab horizonte] »; apud alios}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}etiam \ArabicRun{مطالع الكوكب في التَّوَسُّط}. — \ArabicRun{مطالع البروج في وَسَط السماء} \textit{signorum culminatio, si-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{gnorum transitus per meridianum superiorem}, {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{1}, {\arabicfont ٣٩}\textsubscript{8, 11}, {\arabicfont ١٩٨}\textsubscript{21}. — \ArabicRun{مطالع البروج}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{فيما بَيْنَ الأوْتاد في ارْباع الفلك} \textit{arcus circuli aequinoctialis directionem astrologicam}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(\ArabicRun{تسيير}) \textit{computans, arcus aequatoris caelestis respondens angulo horario quem data}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{stella conficit ut a loco suo pristino ad quemlibet circulum positionis datum perve-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0792-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{niat}, {\arabicfont ١٩٨} seqq. — \ArabicRun{مطالع الزَّوَال} vel \ArabicRun{مطالع القُبَّة} \textit{ascensiones rectae ab initio Capricorni}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الكَبِير} \textit{Dubhe}, \textgreek{α} \textit{Ursae maioris}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٦}. — \ArabicRun{ظَهْرُ الفَرَس} \textgreek{β} \textit{Pegasi}, {\arabicfont ٢٧٧}. — \ArabicRun{ظُهُورٌ} \textgreek{φάσις}, \textit{appa-}}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ritio} planetarum e radiis Solis, {\arabicfont ١٧٦}–{\arabicfont ١٧٨}.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0793-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L008}{}{\ArabicRun{عجم} — \ArabicRun{سَنَةٌ عَجَمِيَّةٌ} \textit{annus Iulianus} Byzantinorum et Syrorum ab initio Septembris aut Octo-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}bris computatus, {\arabicfont ١٠٠}\textsubscript{16}; cfr. \Name{al-Ya‘qūbī}, \textit{Hist.}, ed. Houtsma, II, 129, qui narrat Muḥam-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{21.28pt}mad mortuum esse « mense peregrinorum » (\ArabicRun{شهر العجم}) ādhār i. e. Martio. Eadem voce}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Arabes occidentales, quibus \ArabicRun{عَجَمٌ} sunt Christiani Hispaniae (2), designant \textit{annum Iulianum}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}nostrum a Ianuarii initio computatum, ut apparet ex exemplis I, 204 et II, 202 n.; ergo}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{شهر...العجمي} de mensibus Christiano–Latinis apud \Name{Ibn} \Name{Ǵubayr}, ed. Wright, {\arabicfont ٣٠}\textsubscript{10}, {\arabicfont ٢٨٤}\textsubscript{11}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0793-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L014}{}{\ArabicRun{عَدَدٌ} \textgreek{ὁ ἀριϑμός, οἱ ἀριϑμοί}, \textit{argumentum} tabularum mathematicarum, sensu collectivo et ma-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}scul. generis usurpatur; exempla innumera, ut {\arabicfont ٤١}\textsubscript{11} \ArabicRun{فان كان تفاضل العدد بدرجة ... وان}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{كان تفاضله بعشر درجات}; {\arabicfont ٢١٧}\textsubscript{18} \ArabicRun{ونعلَم على كم من العدد المقسوم في مسطرة ط م} « et sciamus}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L017}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« quantos numerorum in regula HM distributorum ... ». V. \ArabicRun{سطر}.}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0793-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L018}{}{\ArabicRun{تَعْدِيلٌ} \textit{interpolatio} tabularum mathematicarum, {\arabicfont ٣٣}\textsubscript{3, 13, 15}, {\arabicfont ١٢٥}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ١٤١}\textsubscript{18}; ac ita \Name{Ḥabash} saepe}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(f. 17,r. et 150,v., \ArabicRun{عَدَّلَ} \textit{interpolando deducere} quantitatem \textit{a tabulis}), ceterique. Plenius}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Oloug} \Name{Beg}, 57-58 text., \ArabicRun{تعديل ما بين السطرَيْن} (in vers. « équation de l’interligne »).}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L021}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{b}) \textgreek{Διευκρίνησις}, \textit{actio aequandi}, i. e. convertendi medium motum in verum, {\arabicfont ٦٧}\textsubscript{22}. \textit{c}) \textgreek{Προ}-}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{σϑαφαίρεσις}, \textit{aequatio} astronomica, i. e. quantitas addenda aut subducenda mediis valori-}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}bus quos tabulae praebent ut veri fiant, ergo: \ArabicRun{تَعْدِيلٌ} \textgreek{προσϑαφαίρεσις τῆς ἀνωμαλίας}, \textit{aequa-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{tio centri} Solis et planetarum, {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ٧٣}\textsubscript{10} (ubi, et {\arabicfont ٧٤}\textsubscript{14}, definitur \ArabicRun{اختلاف الحركة}), {\arabicfont ١٠٧} etc.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{التعديل الأَوْسَط} \textgreek{προσϑαφαίρεσις μήκους}, \textit{media aequatio centri} planetarum, i. e. in circum-}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}ferentia aequantis, {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ٧٣}\textsubscript{14}, {\arabicfont ٩٥}\textsubscript{16, 17}; \ArabicRun{التعديل الاوسط المعدَّل بانحراف فلك التدوير}, i. q.}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{تعديل الحاصة والمركز}, {\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{22}. \ArabicRun{تعديل الأيَّام بِلَيَالِيهَا} \textit{aequatio temporis}, {\arabicfont ١٠٨}\textsubscript{24} et II, 61-64. \ArabicRun{تعديل}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الحاصّة} \textgreek{πρόσνευσις τοῦ τῆς σελήνης ἐπικύκλου}, \textit{aequatio anomaliae} Lunae, {\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{5} et II, 78-83}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}(cfr. 224-25). \ArabicRun{تعديل الحاصّة والمركز} \textgreek{ζωδιακὴ ἀνωμαλία}, \textit{aequatio centri} planetarum, {\arabicfont ٩٥}\textsubscript{11, 21},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٩٧}\textsubscript{12, 15}, {\arabicfont ١٧٢} plur., II, 108-137 (cfr. 239-41). \ArabicRun{التعديل المجموع الكلّيّ} \textgreek{αἱ συγκρινόμεναι ἀνωμα}-}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{λίαι}, \textit{summa aequationum omnium} planetae, II, 108-137. \ArabicRun{التعديل المُرَكَّب} \textgreek{διπλῆ ἀνωμαλία},}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{aequatio tota} Lunae, i. e. aggregatus aequationis simplicis et secundae inaequalitatis,}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{13, 14}. \ArabicRun{التعديل المُفْرَد} \textgreek{προσϑαφαίρεσις τῆς ἁπλῆς ἀνωμαλίας, προσϑαφαίρεσις ἀπογείου τοῦ ἐκ}-}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{κέντρου}, \textit{aequatio simplex} Lunae, {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{13}, {\arabicfont ١١٢}\textsubscript{11}, II, 78-83. — \ArabicRun{مُعَدَّلٌ} \textgreek{διευκρινημένος} \textit{aequatus},}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}ope (\ArabicRun{ب}) alicuius aequationis \textit{correctus}, qua re saepe i. q. \textit{verus} (syn. \ArabicRun{مقوَّم}); ergo \ArabicRun{الحاصّة}}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0793-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{المعدَّلة} \textgreek{ἡ διευκρινημένη ἀνωμαλία}, \textit{anomalia aequata} Lunae et planetarum, {\arabicfont ٩٨}\textsubscript{1}, {\arabicfont ١١٢} ter,}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L001}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ١٧٢}\textsubscript{6} (ubi defin.); \ArabicRun{البُعْد المعدَّل} \textit{distantia} zenithalis Lunae \textit{correcta} propter parallaxim}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L002}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}altitudinis, {\arabicfont ١١٨}\textsubscript{12, 13}; \ArabicRun{المَسِير المعدَّل} \textit{motus synodicus} Lunae \textit{aequatus ob parallaxim lon-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L003}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{gitudinis}, {\arabicfont ١٦٣} pluries; etc. — \ArabicRun{مُعَدِّلُ النَّهار} (subaud. \ArabicRun{دائرة} vel \ArabicRun{فَلَك}) \textgreek{ὁ ἰσημερινός}, \textit{circulus}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{aequinoctialis}, \textit{aequator caelestis}, non terrestris, persaepe; false \Name{Dozy} vocales \ArabicRun{معدَّل}}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{21.28pt}recepit et « ligne équinoxiale » vertit. — \ArabicRun{ساعة الاعتدال} vel \ArabicRun{ساعة معتدلة} \textgreek{ὁμαλὴ} vel \textgreek{ἴση} vel}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textgreek{ἰσημερινὴ ὥρα}, \textit{hora aequinoctialis} quae 15° aequatoris complectitur, {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{12}, {\arabicfont ٤٢}, {\arabicfont ٤٣}\textsubscript{9}, {\arabicfont ٤٥}\textsubscript{15} etc.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L007}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— \ArabicRun{مَشْرِق الاعتدال} vel \ArabicRun{مَطْلَع الاعتدال} \textit{ortus aequinoctialis}, \textit{punctum E horizontis}, seu}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L008}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}intersectio orientalis horizontis et aequatoris caelestis, {\arabicfont ٣٠}\textsubscript{18}, {\arabicfont ٣٦}\textsubscript{14}, {\arabicfont ٣٧}\textsubscript{20}; eius oppositum}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L009}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{مغرب الاعتدال}, {\arabicfont ٣٦}\textsubscript{14}. — \ArabicRun{النَّهار المُعْتَدِل} \textgreek{ἡ ἰσημερινὴ ἡμέρα}, \textit{dies aequinoctialis} {\arabicfont ٣٠}\textsubscript{12}, {\arabicfont ٢١}\textsubscript{13}.}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L010}{10}{\hspace*{42.56pt}Quae \Name{Dozy}, II, 103,\textit{b}, s v. \ArabicRun{تعديل} habet, ita sunt emendanda: \ArabicRun{تعديل}, apud Arabes}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L011}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Occidentis, \textit{supputatio locorum siderum} ex tabulis astronomicis, vel \textit{ephemeridum cae-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L012}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{lestium} (almanachorum) \textit{constructio} (1); \ArabicRun{اهل التعديل} \textit{qui caelestes ephemerides confi-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L013}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{ciunt}; \ArabicRun{تعديل القِرانات الانتقاليّة} \textit{supputatio mediarum coniunctionum Saturni et Iovis},}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L014}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}quae, singulis 240 annis, ab una in alteram triplicitatem transferuntur (2). Titulus libri}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L015}{15}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\Name{Ibn} \Name{al-Bannā‛}, \ArabicRun{منهاج الطالب في تعديل الكواكب} est « Via quaerentis loca (vera et me-}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« dia) astrorum per tabulas determinare ».}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L017}{}{\ArabicRun{العَذْرَاءُ} \textgreek{ἡ Παρϑένος}, constellatio \textit{Virginis}, {\arabicfont ٢٦٠} (in catalogo fixarum ex Almagesto; sed alibi sem-}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}per \ArabicRun{السنبلة}). V. \Name{Lane}.}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L019}{}{\ArabicRun{عرض} — \ArabicRun{حركة العرض} \textgreek{ἡ κατὰ πλάτος κίνησις} vel \textgreek{πάροδος}, \textit{motus latitudinis} Lunae, \textit{argumentum}}
\par\vspace{2.64pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{latitudinis} Lunae, saepe (cfr. II, 227).}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L021}{}{\ArabicRun{عرف} — \ArabicRun{عُرْفُ الأَسَدِ} est \textit{4 Comae}, {\arabicfont ٢٥٩}; versio Graeci nominis totius constellationis (\textgreek{ὁ Πλόκαμος}).}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Iuxta \Name{Ibn} \Name{Qutaybah} apud \Name{Sprenger}, Journ. of the Asiat. Soc. of Bengal, t. XVII,}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L023}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}1848, p. 678, idem quod \ArabicRun{الخَرَاتَانِ}, \ArabicRun{الزُّبْرَتَانِ}, \ArabicRun{زُبْرَةُ الأَسَد} (\textgreek{δ} \textit{et} \textgreek{ϑ} \textit{Leonis}), v. \Name{Dorn}, \textit{Drei Instr.},}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}54 et \Name{Dozy}, II, 116,\textit{b}. Sed fortasse Ibn Qutaybah simili significatione vocum \ArabicRun{زبرة} et \ArabicRun{عرف}}
\par\vspace{2.10pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}deceptus est.}
\par\vspace{2.73pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L026}{}{\ArabicRun{عرقب} — \ArabicRun{عُرْقُوبُ الرَّامِي} \textgreek{β} \textit{Sagittarii} {\arabicfont ٢٧٨} (p. {\arabicfont ٢٦٢} falso alii stellae nomen apponitur, cfr. II, 163).}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L027}{}{\ArabicRun{عُرْوَةٌ} \textit{ansa} astrolabii, {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{17}; cfr. \Name{Dorn}, 27; \Name{Dozy}, II, 122,\textit{b}; \textit{Libros del saber de astrono-}}
\par\vspace{2.37pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L028}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{mia}, II, 261 (Libros dell astrolabio llano, lib. II, cap. I): « ... la armella redonda en que}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L029}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« ponen la cuerda. porque es sobre las otras partes dél. Et llámanla los aráuigos \textit{alhelca}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« [\ArabicRun{الحَلْقَة}]. que quier dezir \textit{armella}. Et algunos dellos la nombran \textit{alhilaca} [\ArabicRun{العلاقة}]. que}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L031}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« quier tanto dezir cuemo \textit{colgadero}. porque cuelgan della ell astrolábio quando toman}
\par\vspace{2.55pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« la altura. Et dizen á la armella pequenna en que está metida esta otra. \textit{alharua}}
\par\vspace{1.92pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« [\ArabicRun{العروة}]. que quier dezir lazo ».}
\par\vspace{2.91pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0794-E06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0794-body-L034}{}{\ArabicRun{عَشَرَاتٌ} \textit{decades}, {\arabicfont ٤١}\textsubscript{5}, {\arabicfont ٢٢٥}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ٢٢٦}\textsubscript{1}; II, 58. — \ArabicRun{العَاشِرُ} \textgreek{μεσουράνημα}, \textit{medium Caelum}, intersectio}

\par\vspace{2.73pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L016}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}i. q. \ArabicRun{مراتب العَظَمَة} ({\arabicfont ٢٤٥}–{\arabicfont ٢٧٤}), \ArabicRun{عِظَمٌ} ({\arabicfont ٢٧٥} sqq.).}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L017}{}{\ArabicRun{قَدَمٌ} pl. \ArabicRun{أَقْدَامٌ}, vox Albatenio ignota, \textit{pars} gnomonis, quarum gnomon 7 aut 6 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ aut 6 $\qfrac{2}{3}$}
\par\vspace{2.24pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L018}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}complectitur, I, 182. — \ArabicRun{مُقَدَّمٌ} \textgreek{προηγούμενος}, \textit{anterior, occidentalior}, punctum aut sidus}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L019}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}cuius long. minor longitudine alterius, saepissime {\arabicfont ٢٤٥}–{\arabicfont ٢٧٤}. — \ArabicRun{مُقَدَّمُ الذِّرَاعَيْن} \textit{Castor}, \textgreek{α} \textit{Ge-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L020}{20}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{minorum}, {\arabicfont ١٨٨}\textsubscript{22}. — \ArabicRun{مقدَّم الكَلْب} v. \ArabicRun{كلب}.}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L021}{}{\ArabicRun{مُقَارَنَةٌ} \textgreek{ἡ σύνοδος}, \textit{coniunctio} planetarum inter se aut cum Sole aut cum Luna, {\arabicfont ١٧٦}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ١٩٤}\textsubscript{8, 12, 17, 21};}
\par\vspace{2.28pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L022}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}i. q. \ArabicRun{اِقْتِرَانٌ}, {\arabicfont ١٩٤}\textsubscript{18, 19, 20}. — Coniunctionem Solis cum Luna semper \ArabicRun{اجتماع} dicit.}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L023}{}{\ArabicRun{قَسَمَ} i, ut mos est mathematicorum (3), constr. cum \ArabicRun{ب}, non cum accus. partium in quas res}
\par\vspace{2.50pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L024}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}dividitur; \ArabicRun{بِنِصفينِ}, {\arabicfont ٣٥}\textsubscript{5}, {\arabicfont ٣٦}\textsubscript{1, 6}, {\arabicfont ٨٩}\textsubscript{5}, {\arabicfont ١٣٥}\textsubscript{12}, {\arabicfont ٢١٠}\textsubscript{6}; \ArabicRun{بأربعة اقسام}, {\arabicfont ٨}\textsubscript{8}, {\arabicfont ٢٧}\textsubscript{13}; \ArabicRun{بتسعين}, {\arabicfont ١٣٤}\textsubscript{21}; etc.}
\par\vspace{2.22pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L025}{25}{\hspace*{21.28pt}Sed \ArabicRun{ونقسمها أَرْبَاعًا متساوية}, {\arabicfont ٢١٠}\textsubscript{6-7}, {\arabicfont ٢١١}\textsubscript{ult.} Item cum \ArabicRun{ب} construitur \ArabicRun{جَزَّأَ}, {\arabicfont ٨}\textsubscript{5}. — VII, cum}
\par\vspace{2.43pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L026}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الى} partium in quas res dividitur, {\arabicfont ٨} ter, etc. Tamen {\arabicfont ٣٥}\textsubscript{9} \ArabicRun{وتنقسم الدائرة بهذين الخطّيْن ارباعًا}.}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L027}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}— Cfr. \Name{Friedlaender}, 91,\textit{b} ubi constructiones cum \ArabicRun{الى}, \ArabicRun{ل}, \ArabicRun{ب}, \ArabicRun{على} occurrunt.}
\par\vspace{2.51pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L028}{}{\ArabicRun{قُطْبٌ} \textit{polus} astrolabii, \textit{axis}, {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{14, 16}, {\arabicfont ٢١٦}\textsubscript{17}, {\arabicfont ٢١٧}\textsubscript{4}.}
\par\vspace{2.32pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L029}{}{\ArabicRun{قُطْرٌ} \textit{hypotenusa}, {\arabicfont ٣٢} quinquies, {\arabicfont ٢٠٦}\textsubscript{21, 23}; cfr. I, 181, n. 2. Inde \ArabicRun{قطر القمر} \textit{distantia geocentrica}}
\par\vspace{2.33pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L030}{30}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{Lunae} in figura quadam, {\arabicfont ١١٧}\textsubscript{6, 12}. — \textit{Diagonalis} quadrilateri, {\arabicfont ٥٧}\textsubscript{14, 16}, {\arabicfont ٥٩}\textsubscript{1, 17}.}
\par\vspace{2.23pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0798-E13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L031}{}{\ArabicRun{قَطَعَ} a, \textit{pertransire}, {\arabicfont ١٨}\textsubscript{1} \ArabicRun{دائرة وسط السماء القاطعة لقُطْبَيْ فلك معدِّل النهار} « circulus meridianus}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L032}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« per binos aequatoris caelestis polos transiens »; {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{8} \ArabicRun{القاطعة بين الشمال والجنوب} « per}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L033}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}« punctum N et punctum S transiens »; {\arabicfont ٢١٣}\textsubscript{5} \ArabicRun{القاطعة فيما بين المشرق والمغرب}. — \textit{Percur-}}
\par\vspace{2.46pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L034}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\textit{rere, perambulare, spatium conficere}, {\arabicfont ٢٢}\textsubscript{10}, {\arabicfont ٢٤}\textsubscript{2, 19}, {\arabicfont ٦٤}–{\arabicfont ٦٥} pluries, {\arabicfont ٦٦}\textsubscript{6}, {\arabicfont ٦٩}\textsubscript{ult.}, {\arabicfont ٧٢}\textsubscript{17}, {\arabicfont ١٠٧}\textsubscript{4},}
\par\vspace{2.19pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L035}{35}{\hspace*{21.28pt}{\arabicfont ٢١٤}\textsubscript{9} et loco spurio {\arabicfont ٢٢١}–{\arabicfont ٢٢٢}. Similiter \Name{aṭ-Ṭabarī}, I, 19, l. 13: \ArabicRun{وان الساعات انما هي قطع}}
\par\vspace{2.30pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L036}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الشمس والقمر درجات الفلك}; I, 22, l. 18–20: \ArabicRun{شهور اهل الدنيا التي تُعَدّ ساعاتها وايّامها بقطع}}
\par\vspace{2.26pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0798-body-L037}{}{\hspace*{21.28pt}\ArabicRun{الشمس والقمر درج الفلك}. Cfr. \Name{Dozy}, II, 367,\textit{a} (ex Pseudo-Wâkidî).}
\par\vspace{7.15pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5.60pt}
